### [1] [Emptiness formation in the Lieb-Liniger gas: hydrodynamic instantons and a conjectured rate function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26640)
*Boris A. Khanikati,Alexander G. Abanov*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究一维Lieb-Liniger排斥玻色气体基态中的空区形成概率（EFP），针对宏观空区间长度2R，提出一个无参数积分方程来描述其速率函数f(γ₀)，并从精确的Fredholm行列式表示出发给出该核的形式推导，同时通过数值方法验证了该猜想在多个极限和耦合强度范围内的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空区形成概率是描述量子多体系统关联性质的重要物理量，在Lieb-Liniger模型中其大尺度行为由速率函数决定。此前缺乏对该速率函数的统一描述，尤其在中间耦合区域缺乏精确解析结果，因此需要提出一个普适的解析框架。

Method: 从EFP的精确对偶场Fredholm行列式表示出发，形式推导出猜想的热力学极限积分方程核；通过Tonks-Girardeau极限、弱耦合极限及其一阶修正验证猜想；同时利用Lieb-Liniger流体动力学作用量的数值极小化方法，在超过四个数量级的耦合强度范围内计算速率函数并对比。

Result: 猜想正确复现了Tonks-Girardeau和弱耦合极限以及两个极限中独立得到的首阶修正；数值最小化结果显示与猜想在百分之几的误差内吻合，跨越超过四个数量级的耦合范围；数值计算还给出了相应的空区瞬子及其星形（astroid-like）真空区域结构。

Conclusion: 提出的无参数积分方程为Lieb-Liniger气体EFP的速率函数提供了一个统一的解析描述，该描述在多个极限和广泛耦合范围内均得到验证，但严格的数学推导仍有待完成。

Abstract: We study the emptiness formation probability (EFP) in the ground state of the repulsive one-dimensional Lieb-Liniger Bose gas. For a macroscopic empty interval of length $2R$, its leading asymptotic behavior is described by a rate function $f(γ_0)$, defined by $-\log P(R)\sim(ρ_0R)^2f(γ_0)$, where $ρ_0$ is the mean density and $γ_0$ is the dimensionless interaction strength. We propose a parameter-free integral equation for $f(γ_0)$. Starting from the exact dual-field Fredholm-determinant representation of the EFP, we show how the conjectured kernel arises formally and identify the uniform asymptotic statement that remains to be proven for a rigorous derivation. The conjecture reproduces the Tonks-Girardeau and weak-coupling limits, as well as the first correction obtained independently in both limits. It also agrees at the few-percent level with numerical minimization of the Lieb-Liniger hydrodynamic action over more than four orders of magnitude in coupling. The numerical calculation yields the corresponding emptiness instantons and their astroid-like vacuum regions.

</details>


### [2] [Vortex lattices in coupled one-dimensional Bose-Einstein condensates with a synthetic magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26966)
*Holly A. J. Middleton-Spencer,Rose Davies,David G. Reid,Anna Dalmasso,Theodore Kazanas,Hannah M. Price*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合一维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体在合成磁场下的平均场基态，揭示了从少腿梯子到扩展涡旋晶格的转变，发现周期边界条件倾向于形成类似Abrikosov晶格的交错涡旋阵列，而开放边界在小尺寸系统中将涡旋限制在合成方向中心附近的行中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探索连续-离散量子流体的有限尺寸效应和边界条件如何决定涡旋的涌现及其空间组织，为隧道耦合原子线或合成维度实验提供理论基础。

Method: 采用平均场方法研究耦合导线模型，该模型具有一个连续和一个离散空间方向，通过改变导线数量在少腿梯子与扩展涡旋晶格之间进行受控插值；对两线系统分析涡旋样、偏置密度和类Meissner态，并数值模拟不同导线数目下周期和开放边界条件的作用。

Result: 两线系统揭示了有限纵向尺寸对基态转变的影响；增加导线数目后，基态演化为扩展涡旋晶格。周期边界条件可促成类似Abrikosov晶格的同号涡旋交错阵列，而开放边界在小尺寸系统中将涡旋约束在合成方向中心附近的行内。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸效应和边界条件显著影响连续-离散量子流体中涡旋的涌现与空间组织，这些发现对隧道耦合原子线或合成维度等未来实验具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We investigate the mean-field ground states of coupled one-dimensional Bose-Einstein condensates subject to a synthetic magnetic field. The resulting interacting coupled-wire model has one continuous and one discrete spatial direction, providing a controlled way to interpolate between the physics of few-leg ladders and extended vortex lattices. For two wires, we study the vortex-like, biased-density, and Meissner-like states, exploring how the finite longitudinal size of the system modifies the transitions between them. Increasing the number of wires, the ground state evolves towards an extended vortex lattice. We find numerically that periodic boundary conditions in the discrete direction can favour staggered arrays of like-signed vortices resembling an Abrikosov lattice, while open boundaries in small finite-size systems confine the vortices into rows near the centre of the synthetic direction. Our results explore how finite size effects and boundary conditions govern the emergence and spatial organisation of vortices in continuous-discrete quantum fluids, with relevance to future experiments in tunnel-coupled atomic wires or with synthetic dimensions.

</details>


### [3] [Crossing the Rotational Sound Barrier in a Quantum Solvent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27030)
*Baptiste Coquinot,Giacomo Bighin,Mikhail Lemeshko,Ragheed Alhyder*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究超流体环境中被驱动的旋转分子（如光学离心机中的转子），发现转子可在特征谐波频率以下保持局域化，该频率可超过浴激发能，从而进入超快旋转杂质领域。在共转坐标系中，浴激发经历旋转多普勒频移，产生动量分辨共振。文中识别出耗散旋转声屏障，分子在此处共振发射浴激发并与周围介质发生强角动量交换，最终建立了量子溶剂中驱动转子的动力学相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流体中的分子是研究杂质物理的理想实验平台，但现有角动量（angulon）理论主要处理平衡态或慢旋转情形，对驱动频率与浴动力学相当的超快旋转杂质缺乏理论理解，因此需要建立非平衡驱动的理论框架。

Method: 基于光学离心机这一实验平台，采用共转坐标系描述，引入旋转多普勒效应修正浴激发谱，结合角动量分辨的共振分析，构建驱动转子在量子溶剂中的动力学相图。

Result: 发现转子在特征谐波频率以下保持局域化，该频率可超过浴的激发能量；在共转框架中浴激发出现旋转多普勒频移，产生平衡态angulon理论中不存在的角动量分辨共振；识别出耗散旋转声屏障，分子在此时共振发射浴激发并发生强角动量交换。

Conclusion: 建立了量子溶剂中驱动转子的动力学相图，为研究快速驱动的旋转杂质在量子多体系统中的行为提供了通用平台，并扩展了angulon理论至非平衡驱动区域。

Abstract: Molecules embedded in superfluids provide an experimentally controllable platform for investigating impurity physics. Here, we investigate a driven molecule rotating in a superfluid environment, including helium and Bose--Einstein condensates, at rotation frequencies similar to the bath dynamics. Within the experimentally relevant platform of an optical centrifuge, we show that the rotor remains localized up to a characteristic harmonic frequency that can realistically exceed the excitation energies of the bath, enabling access to ultrafast rotating impurities. In the co-rotating frame, the bath excitations experience a rotational Doppler shift, generating angular-momentum-resolved resonances absent in equilibrium angulon theory. We identify a dissipative rotational sound barrier at which the molecule resonantly emits bath excitations and undergoes strong angular momentum exchange with the surrounding medium. Overall, we establish the dynamical phase diagram of the driven rotor in a quantum solvent and introduce a generic platform for investigating fast driven rotating impurities in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [4] [The heavy Fermi polaron I: the Lithium-Cesium experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27322)
*Michael Rautenberg,Tobias Krom,Eleonora Lippi,Lauriane Chomaz,Matthias Weidemüller*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于研究费米极化子的实验平台：将约千个¹³³Cs玻色杂质浸入约2×10⁵个⁶Li原子组成的深度简并费米气体（T/T_F≈0.2）中，通过费什巴赫共振调节种间相互作用，并利用无相对动量转移的光学拉曼光谱进行注入谱学，从而创建费米极化子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在构建一个新的实验平台，利用¹³³Cs与⁶Li之间的大质量失配，探索此前无法到达的费米极化子新参数区域。传统费米极化子实验中杂质与费米海的质量比较为接近，而该系统的大质量比（~22）为研究强耦合极子物理提供了全新途径。

Method: 实验采用约千个¹³³Cs玻色杂质浸入约2×10⁵个⁶Li原子组成的深度简并费米气体（T/T_F≈0.2）中，系统处于单一超精细态。通过费什巴赫共振实现种间相互作用的可调谐，利用无相对动量转移的光学拉曼光谱执行注入谱学来创建费米极化子。

Result: 该平台成功实现了费米极化子的创建与光谱学表征，并凭借¹³³Cs-⁶Li体系的大质量失配，提供了进入以往实验无法探索的费米极化子新参数区域的途径。

Conclusion: 该实验平台为研究大质量失配极限下的费米极化子物理提供了新的工具，有望揭示强耦合与高质量比条件下极化子的新行为。

Abstract: We present details of an experimental platform for studying Fermi polarons in a quantum-gas mixture. The system consists of about a thousand bosonic $^{133}$Cs impurities immersed in a deeply degenerate Fermi gas ($T/T_F \sim 0.2$) of approximately $2\times 10^5$ $^6$Li atoms in a single hyperfine state, with interspecies interactions tunable via a Feshbach resonance. Using optical Raman spectroscopy without relative momentum transfer, we perform injection spectroscopy and thereby create the Fermi polaron. Owing to the large mass imbalance between the two species, the setup provides access to previously unexplored regimes of Fermi polarons.

</details>

### [5] [Statistics as a local phase: crystalline order and quench dynamics of emergent dimers in Ising gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26264)
*Umberto Borla,Riccardo Cioli,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究2+1维Z2格点规范理论强耦合极限下，玻色子或费米子微观统计如何在其束缚形成的复合二聚体（dimer）中存活。研究发现底层物质的统计性质完全编码在一个局部跃迁相位φ中（玻色子为0，费米子为π），而相互作用与统计无关。通过将φ视为连续参数，利用张量网络方法绘制了基态相图，发现φ本身驱动二聚体超流与二聚体电荷密度波之间的相变，磁耦合使相邻二聚体结合成共振对，与二聚体间排斥相互作用竞争，并识别出二聚体对结晶为有序共振plaquette图案的新型有隙相。最后提出了一种淬火协议，使相同的二聚体构型根据其组成粒子的统计性质以显著不同方式演化，为探测二聚体内部结构和有序相提供了动力学探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解当微观粒子具有不同统计性质（玻色/费米）时，它们被束缚成复合玻色子（如二聚体）后，原始统计信息是否以及如何保留下来。这是一个关于涌现复合粒子的统计性质的fundamental问题。

Method: 在2+1维Z2格点规范理论的强耦合极限下，将束缚电荷对映射为有效二聚体模型。将统计相位φ作为连续插值参数，采用张量网络方法（tensor-network methods）计算基态相图。同时设计淬火协议作为动力学探针。

Result: 识别出多种相：二聚体超流体、二聚体电荷密度波、以及由磁耦合驱动形成的新型有隙共振plaquette晶体相（二聚体对结晶为有序共振plaquette模式）。φ驱动超流到电荷密度波的相变，磁耦合与二聚体间排斥竞争。淬火协议下相同二聚体构型依统计性质呈现显著不同的实时演化。

Conclusion: 微观粒子的统计性质（玻色/费米）可以通过单一局部位跃迁相位φ在复合束缚态中完整保留，尽管复合粒子本身表现为玻色子。这一统计编码可以通过动力学淬火探针在实时信号中检测，为在量子模拟器中探测复合粒子内部结构提供了可行方案。

Abstract: How does the Bose or Fermi statistics of microscopic particles survive when confinement binds them into emergent bosonic composites? We address this question in the strong-coupling limit of a $2+1$D $\mathbb{Z}_2$ lattice gauge theory, where charges are confined into tightly bound pairs that can be described by an effective dimer model. We find that the statistics of the underlying matter is encoded entirely in a single local hopping phase $\varphi$ -$0$ for bosons, $π$ for fermions- while interactions remain statistics-independent. Treating $\varphi$ as a continuous parameter that interpolates between the two, we map the ground-state phase diagram with the help of tensor-network methods. The angle $\varphi$ itself drives a transition between a dimer-superfluid and dimer charge density wave state, while the magnetic coupling binds neighboring dimers into resonating pairs, in competition with the inter-dimer repulsion. We identify a novel gapped phase in which dimer pairs crystallize into an ordered pattern of resonating plaquettes. Finally, we propose a quench protocol under which identical dimer configurations evolve in markedly different ways depending on the statistics of their constituents. This provides a dynamical probe of the internal structure of dimers, and detects ordered phases through real time signatures, within reach of simulators that natively realize bosonic degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [6] [Time and Momentum Resolved Tunneling Spectroscopy of Floquet dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26270)
*Lucas Q. Silveira,Adrian E. Feiguin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种时间和动量分辨的隧穿光谱协议，用于探测Floquet驱动量子系统在非高频极限下的瞬时能谱，无需重构完整的时间依赖格林函数，并推广到强关联系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对周期驱动量子系统的处理通常简化为高频极限，忽略驱动开启瞬态过程中的非绝热现象，缺乏超出高频极限的能谱探测手段。

Method: 引入时间和动量分辨的隧穿光谱协议，在非相互作用费米模型上验证后，借助时间依赖密度矩阵重整化群（tDMRG）技术推广到强关联系统。

Result: 成功捕捉到新兴效应和非绝热现象；给出了共振驱动下隙内态涌现的微观描述，并初步探索了有限温度下的对应情形。

Conclusion: 该协议为超出高频极限的Floquet系统提供了有效的瞬时能谱探测途径，并对共振驱动产生的隙内态提供了微观理解。

Abstract: Periodically driven quantum systems provide a powerful route to engineer novel states of matter by controlling their effective Hamiltonians through external fields. However, most treatments are usually simplified by considering the high-frequency limit, and do not account for the processes taking place in the transient regime during the onset of the drive. In this work, we introduce a time and momentum resolved tunneling spectroscopy protocol to probe the instantaneous energy spectrum of Floquet-driven systems beyond the high-frequency regime, capturing both emergent effects and non-adiabatic phenomena without requiring explicit reconstruction of the full time-dependent Green's function. We benchmark the method on driven non-interacting fermionic models, and then generalize the approach to strongly correlated systems with the aid of time-dependent density matrix renormalization group techniques. We also provide a microscopic description to the emergence of in-gap states under resonant driving, and briefly explore its finite temperature analog.

</details>


### [7] [Dynamical signatures of deconfined spinons in dimerized sawtooth chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26347)
*Nishan Ranabhat,Brandon B. Le,Seung-Hun Lee,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过U(1)对称的DMRG和TDVP方法，系统地研究了锯齿链上海森堡模型的基态性质和零温动力学结构因子S^zz(q,ω)，涵盖了二聚化相、精确可解的VBS点及其上下相边界附近的全部参数区域。研究发现谱在所有区域均为宽的连续谱，由低能边界的亮带主导，并鉴定为去禁闭的双自旋子（kink-antikink）连续谱，与Ti³⁺氟化七角格材料相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解锯齿链海森堡模型中二聚化相内的分数化激发（自旋子）的微观图像，特别是在精确可解VBS点之外的更广泛参数区域中，连续谱边界和非弹性散射强度的物理起源。

Method: 使用U(1)对称的密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算平衡态性质，使用含时变分原理（TDVP）计算零温动力学结构因子；通过闭式自旋子色散关系确定连续谱边界，并在约束希尔伯特空间中进行有限分离的双kink计算以复现强度分布。

Result: 在所有研究的参数区间（下相边界附近、精确VBS点、上相边界附近）内，DSF均为宽连续谱，由下边缘的亮带主导，单triplon能量处的强度被抑制。闭式自旋子色散能准确固定连续谱边缘并追踪主导带，有限分离双kink计算成功复现了测量到的强度分布。

Conclusion: 锯齿链二聚化相的激发谱被鉴定为去禁闭的kink-antikink双自旋子连续谱，为分数化激发提供了微观图像。该结果对具有强各向异性交换相互作用、可形成锯齿链构建块的Ti³⁺氟化七角格材料具有直接参考意义。

Abstract: We study the ground-state properties and dynamical response of the Heisenberg model on the sawtooth chain in and away from the exactly solvable valence-bond-solid (VBS) point. We employ U(1)-symmetric density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) methods to compute equilibrium diagnostics and the zero-temperature dynamical structure factor (DSF) $S^{zz}(q,ω)$ across the dimerized phase, a valence-bond-ordered state in which the two symmetry-equivalent apex--base bonds of each triangle develop unequal spin correlations, probing the approach to the continuous lower phase boundary, the exact VBS point, and the approach to the first-order upper boundary. In every regime, the DSF is a broad continuum dominated by a bright band at its lower edge, with the intensity at the one-triplon energy $ω\simeq J_{AB}$ suppressed. We identify the spectrum as a deconfined two-spinon continuum of kink and antikink domain walls between the two degenerate singlet coverings. Closed-form spinon dispersions fix the continuum edges and track the dominant band across the zone in all three regimes, while an explicit finite-separation two-kink calculation in a constrained Hilbert space reproduces the measured intensity distribution. Our results provide a microscopic picture of fractionalized excitations in the dimerized sawtooth chain and are relevant to the recently discovered Ti$^{3+}$ kagome fluorides, where strongly anisotropic exchange interactions can generate sawtooth-chain building blocks.

</details>


### [8] [Competition between Geometrical Frustration and the Kondo Effect in CePdAl Revealed by High-Resolution Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26534)
*Yusei Shimizu,Shota Nakamura,Yoichi Ikeda,Yohei Kono,Shunichiro Kittaka,Toshiro Sakakibara,Yosikazu Isikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过高灵敏度磁力计研究重费米子化合物CePdAl在超低温（80 mK）下的磁行为，发现清晰的没有磁化平台的亚稳态一级磁转变，表明磁场通过抑制近藤效应和有序矩自旋翻转来解除几何阻挫。研究还发现4.3 T处的非线性磁化率异常，且在反铁磁序被完全压制的磁场区域未观察到非费米液体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CePdAl是一种具有准kagome结构的重费米子化合物，其中几何阻挫与近藤效应竞争。理解磁场如何影响这种竞争对于揭示其磁场诱导的自旋液体态至关重要，因此需要建立精确的热力学约束条件。

Method: 利用QGN模型中的Fock空间碎裂结构：证明基态上的激发解耦为Krylov子空间（由固定数目的粒子和空穴算子张成），并以此推导连续对称破缺的stiffness等于Gaussian流形上的变分值。关键论证是：无穷小相位扭转只将基态耦合到单粒子-单空穴碎片，该碎片恰好与变分流形中基态的切空间重合，从而使变分曲率成为精确值。对于更一般的扰动，只要其作用保持在固定Fock空间碎片内，响应函数即可由对应的少数体扇区精确求解。

Result: 严格证明了QGN模型中自发破缺连续对称性的stiffness精确等于其在Gaussian流形中的变分值，证实了量子多体bootstrap的猜想。此外，建立了在Fock空间碎片保持性扰动下响应函数（包括静态磁化率、光学电导率、动力学结构因子和单粒子Green函数）的精确可解性。

Conclusion: QGN模型代表了一类比传统精确可解模型更强的可解性类别：不仅能获得谱，还能精确计算广泛的动力学响应函数，为量子多体系统的非平衡与响应性质研究提供了新的精确计算平台。

Abstract: Exactly solvable quantum many-body models are rare, and even when their spectra are algebraically organized, dynamical responses generally remain difficult to obtain because they probe an extensive number of excited states. Here we show that quantum geometric nesting (QGN) models admit an unusually strong form of solvability rooted in \emph{Fock-space fragmentation}: excitations on top of the exact frustration-free ground states decouple into Krylov subspaces with a fixed number of particle and hole operators, and hence remain dynamically invariant. Exploiting this structure, we prove that the stiffness of the spontaneously broken continuous symmetry in QGN models is exactly equal to its variational value in the Gaussian manifold, confirming a conjecture from quantum many-body bootstrap~\cite{GaoHanKhalaf2026}. The proof shows that an infinitesimal phase twist couples the ground state only to the one-particle, one-hole fragment, which coincides with the tangent space of the ground state within the variational manifold, thereby making the variational curvature exact. More generally, perturbations whose action remains within a fixed Fock-space fragment have response functions determined exactly by the corresponding few-body sector, enabling exact access to quantities including static susceptibility, optical conductivity, dynamical structure factors, and single-particle Green's functions.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous quantum phase transitions in interacting many-body systems exhibit universal phenomena that are largely independent of microscopic details. An organizing principle distilled from canonical examples such as the magnetic transition in Ising models is that the low-energy physics of transitions between gapped phases is governed by scale-invariant quantum field theories with gapless degrees of freedom at isolated points in momentum space. In this paper, we show that this principle can fail in a radical way: an entire gapless Fermi surface can emerge at a quantum critical point separating two fully gapped insulators. These exotic phase transitions are dubbed \textit{metallogenic quantum critical points} (MGQCP). We construct a controlled family of effective field theories for MGQCPs between distinct fractional Chern insulators and propose a periodically modulated quantum Hall bilayer as a minimal microscopic setting. The critical theory exhibits an unusual mixture of physical signatures: while transport and compressibility follow scaling laws compatible with a conformal field theory, the electron spectral function develops a power-law non-analyticity at the Fermi momentum, characteristic of non-Fermi-liquid metals. We discuss how the concept of MGQCP may shed new light on critical phenomena in moire materials with tunable Chern bands. More generally, our results point to a broader class of quantum critical phenomena in which extended manifolds of gapless excitations emerge in momentum space despite being absent in both neighboring phases.

</details>

For a Wigner matrix, the correlation length diverges at criticality but the susceptibility remains finite, and one-loop replica analysis supports an upper critical dimension of unity. In an exactly solvable spherical variant, the heat capacity jumps at the transition in $d>1$, and correlations remain pinned to their critical form throughout the glass phase, giving generic scale invariance despite the absence of a Goldstone mode.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最大熵原理和均值场近似的概率信息论框架来求解旅行商问题（TSP）。通过将局部约束（每个城市访问一次）和全局约束（单一哈密顿环）分别编码为顶点级约束和全局倾斜项，并引入可微的环惩罚项抑制子回路，算法在多项式时间内收敛到连通回路，在TSPLIB实例上多数达到最优解。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 定义了图上的最大熵概率分布：边成本和度约束生成assignment-like系综，全局硬约束项将系综倾斜向哈密顿环。为使其可计算，推导了关于边占据数的均值场近似，并实现可微的环惩罚项来抑制子环。通过自洽数值迭代，输出不仅包含候选路线，还包含编码竞争边和退化解的概率矩阵。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) asks for the shortest route to visit a set of cities exactly once. It combines a simple local rule - each city must be visited once - with a hard, global constraint- all cities must be traversed within a single cycle. We cast the problem within a probabilistic, information-theoretic framework. The coexistence of local and global constraints is precisely what makes the problem difficult to address in this framework: the local rule can be enforced through vertex-level constraints, whereas the global constraint cannot be captured by independent edge probabilities. We show that this obstacle can be overcome by defining a maximum-entropy probability distribution over graphs, in which edge costs and degree constraints generate an assignment-like ensemble, and a global term, describing the hard constraint, tilts this ensemble toward Hamiltonian cycles. To make the construction tractable, we derive a mean-field approximation in terms of edge occupancies and implement a differentiable cycle penalty that suppresses sub-tours. This leads to a self-consistent numerical procedure whose output is not only a candidate tour but also a probability matrix encoding competing edges and degenerate solutions. We test the method on synthetic ensembles and on TSPLIB instances. The algorithm converges to connected tours in polynomial time, matching the best-known solution in the majority of instances and remaining within a small relative gap otherwise. Beyond its competitive performance, the proposed framework offers a general approach for handling hard constraints while reducing hard combinatorial optimization problems to simpler ones.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究波动-响应关系在粗粒化随机动力学中涌现的程度。作者以旋转随机朗道模型（过阻尼带电布朗粒子在恒定磁场中与旋转环境耦合）为研究对象，利用Martin-Siggia-Rose路径积分证明存在一个实现时间反演动力学的Z₂对称变换，该变换使作用量只改变一个边界项。通过与Crooks涨落定理比较，该边界项被识别为稳态构型间跃迁的熵；耦合外部源后，同一对称性给出联系涨落与响应的Ward恒等式。研究发现这些恒等式完全源于粗粒化随机理论，不固定噪声强度；在施加爱因斯坦关系后，它们与微观吉布斯系综的旋转Kubo-Martin-Schwinger条件所隐含的高温涨落耗散关系一致。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究涨落-响应关系（FDR）在多大程度上仅从粗粒化随机动力学中涌现，而不依赖于系统的额外微观信息，从而厘清粗粒化理论的边界与普适性。

Method: 以可精确求解的旋转随机朗道模型为测试平台，采用Martin-Siggia-Rose路径积分构造对称变换，推导出实现时间反演的动力学的Z₂对称性；通过Crooks涨落定理关联边界项与熵；耦合外源后利用同一对称性导出Ward恒等式，并与旋转Kubo-Martin-Schwinger条件比较。

Result: 证明了粗粒化随机理论中存在实现时间反演的Z₂对称性，导出的Ward恒等式与涨落-响应关系完全由随机动力学涌现，不依赖于噪声强度；仅在施加爱因斯坦关系后，才与微观吉布斯系综的KMS条件所给出的高温FDR一致。

Conclusion: 粗粒化随机动力学本身足以产生特定形式的涨落-响应关系，但要将这些关系与平衡态热力学涨落-耗散定理完全对应，仍需额外的物理输入（如爱因斯坦关系）。该工作澄清了随机粗粒化理论中FDR的涌现机制及其与微观统计力学的关系。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the extent to which fluctuation--response relations emerge from coarse-grained stochastic dynamics alone, and which aspects instead depend on additional information about the system. To address this question, we study the rotating stochastic Landau model, an exactly solvable system describing an overdamped charged Brownian particle in a constant magnetic field, coupled dissipatively to a rotating environment, whose steady state supports circulating probability currents. Using the Martin--Siggia--Rose path integral, we show that there is an emergent $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry transformation that implements the time-reversed dynamics and changes the action by a boundary term. Comparison with the Crooks fluctuation theorem identifies this term with the entropy associated with transitions between steady-state configurations. After coupling the theory to external sources, the same symmetry yields Ward identities relating fluctuations and response. These identities follow entirely from the coarse-grained stochastic theory and do not fix the noise strength. Finally, upon imposing the Einstein relation, we show that they coincide with the high-temperature fluctuation--dissipation relations implied by the rotating Kubo--Martin--Schwinger condition for a microscopic Gibbs ensemble.

### [20] [When dissipative steady states admit thermodynamic occupation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26621)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡稳态（NESS）本质上缺乏热力学占据定律，因为有限定态环流与全局精确的速率比场（rate-ratio field）无法在同一马尔可夫生成元中共存。这构成了建立非平衡热力学统计规律的根本障碍，亟需一种既能保持耗散又允许建立占据定律的理论框架。

Method: (1) 构造扇区分隔（sector-separated）几何，将熵产生态0从条件占据流形中排除但保留在耗散全图中；(2) 在强偏置/快速重置（SR）极限下，使物理返回过程i→0→0*有效马尔可夫化；(3) 通过热力学完备条件流形上的自主再分布（AR）消除残余无效环流，使速率比一形式精确；(4) 进行热力学校准导出X_i=β(Δμ-F_i^cost)及相应的玻尔兹曼型占据概率；(5) 利用全图概率分解P_α=(1-P_0)p_α实现精确因子化。

Result: 在SR极限下，动力学因子趋于1而条件占据趋于指数形式，全图概率趋于简单占据律但耗散保持有限；在AR邻近区域，可积性的损失对残余环流电流是线性的而耗散的损失是二次的。在二元零环秩极限下，占据在保持Δμ偏置下自主重分布，获得反费米-狄拉克定律，成功应用于量子点激光器的热展宽分析。

Conclusion: 该框架为耗散性非平衡稳态中的热力学占据定律提供了建设性的获取条件和失败诊断准则，证明在不牺牲耗散的前提下建立非平衡占据统计是可行的，并给出了具有实际应用价值的反费米-狄拉克分布实例。

Abstract: Non-equilibrium steady states (NESSs) generally lack thermodynamic occupation laws because finite stationary circulation and a globally exact rate-ratio field cannot coexist for the same Markov generator. Here we construct a sector-separated geometry that overcomes this incompatibility without arresting dissipation. Entropy-production exposure-and-separation excludes the entropy-producing state~$0$ from the conditional occupation manifold while retaining it in the dissipative full graph; physical returns $i\to0\to0^\ast$ become effectively Markovian in the strong-bias/rapid-reset (SR) limit. For a thermodynamically complete conditional manifold, autonomous redistribution (AR) eliminates residual futile circulation, making the rate-ratio one-form exact. Thermodynamic calibration gives $X_i=β(Δμ- \mathcal F_i^{\mathrm{cost}})$ and $p_i=e^{X_i}/Z_\mathcal{C}$, with $Z_\mathcal{C}=1+\sum_i e^{X_i}$. Full-graph probabilities factorize exactly as $P_α=(1-P_0)p_α$. In the SR limit, the kinetic factor tends to unity while $p_α\to e^{X_α}/Z_\mathcal C$, yielding $P_α\to p_α$ while finite dissipation persists. Near AR, integrability is lost linearly in residual cycle current whereas dissipation begins quadratically. In the binary zero-cycle-rank limit, occupation redistributes autonomously under maintained $Δμ$ bias, yielding the inverted Fermi--Dirac law, which is applied to thermal smearing in quantum-dot lasers. The framework provides constructive acquisition conditions and failure diagnostics for thermodynamic occupation laws in dissipative NESSs.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作为BnGD现象提供了一种新的解释机制，无需依赖环境异质性假设，扩展了对非平衡统计物理和非高斯扩散现象的理论理解。

Abstract: In Brownian yet non-Gaussian diffusion (BnGD) the mean squared displacement grows linearly in time. However, the displacement statistics do not follow a normal distribution throughout. Typically, they are non-Gaussian at intermediate times, before they cross over to Gaussian in the long-time regime. We demonstrate that nonlinear friction under correctly applied stochastic equilibrium conditions provides an explanation of this phenomenon also for homogeneous environments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: Krylov截断的可靠性时间由输运度量下的因果光锥决定，其break time与τ_m成正比，但在谱被充分解析或激发局域化时截断可以更长久地有效，为实际选择截断维度提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Krylov and Lanczos approximations are used in quantum dynamics, quantum subspace methods, and Hamiltonian learning. A practical question is how long an $m$-dimensional Krylov truncation can be trusted. We argue that this time is fixed by causal propagation on the associated Jacobi chain. The relevant distance is not the Krylov index itself, but the inhomogeneous transport metric $ρ(m,n) = \sum_{j=\min(m,n)}^{\max(m,n)-1} 1/b_j$, where $b_j$ is the Lanczos hopping across the bond $j \leftrightarrow j+1$. We prove a Lieb--Robinson bound in this metric. Its small-weight limit gives the velocity $v_{\rm LR} = 2$, meaning that propagation is exponentially suppressed outside the cone $ρ(m,n) \simeq 2|t|$. The error of a finite Krylov approximation to the return amplitude is a round-trip effect: information has to travel from the probe to the truncation boundary and back. Combining the Lieb--Robinson bound with Duhamel's formula yields a lower bound on the error of the truncated dynamics. For a fixed tolerance $ε$, let the break time $t_\ast(m;ε)$ denote the longest time for which the $m$-dimensional truncation is guaranteed to reproduce the exact return amplitude within error $ε$. We show that $t_\ast(m;ε) \ge τ_m[1-o(1)]$, where $τ_m = ρ(0,m) = \sum_{j<m} 1/b_j$. When the probe spreads along the chain, this lower bound is also tight, so $t_\ast(m) \simeq τ_m$. The situation is different when the probe excites only a localized part of the spectrum, or a part already resolved by the truncation. In this case, essentially no signal reaches the boundary. Beyond a state-dependent Krylov dimension $m_\ast$, the approximation can therefore remain accurate at all times, and the break time is effectively infinite. It must be reformulated as "from the friend's perspective, the friend saw outcome A" or "from Wigner's perspective, the friend reported outcome B." Finally, we present a sparse-deletion binomial PI protocol that guarantees CHSH violation for up to $O(\sqrt n)$ deletion errors. Our results distinguish fundamental limits imposed by loss from those set by explicit measurements without recovery.

Abstract: Private distributed quantum sensing aims to estimate an authorized collective parameter while preventing independent estimation of individual local parameters. Abstract: In open quantum systems, the environment is typically modelled as a collection of harmonic oscillators, whereas realistic environments often exhibit unique non-Markovian effects due to the anharmonicity of the environment. Here we present a framework for modelling anharmonic environments in open quantum systems numerically exactly, in which, via a matching criterion, we map the anharmonic environment onto an effective harmonic one. This results in an effective spectral density that is a collection of shifted bare spectra arising from the non-equidistant energy gaps of the anharmonic environment, along with a unique zero-frequency term that effectively acts as static disorder on the system. This is due to the non-zero variance of the diagonal of the bath coupling operator. In tandem, we develop a framework for investigating the effects of environments comprising a few damped anharmonic modes where the matching criterion fails to work due to the non-Gaussianity of the environment. For both continuous and discrete damped anharmonic environments, this work investigates their unique influence on the system's dynamics, including enhanced non-Markovianity compared to a harmonic approximation, novel effects in the absorption spectrum, and the potential for anharmonic environments to enhance energy transport.

Abstract: Contraction coefficients quantify the loss of distinguishability induced by a channel and provide a strong form of the data-processing inequality. While standard contraction coefficients ignore auxiliary quantum systems, existing extensions based on complete contraction coefficients require the compared states to have identical reference marginals. In this work, we introduce conditional contraction coefficients, a novel family that incorporates arbitrary quantum reference systems by subtracting the distinguishability already present in the reference system. We develop a general framework for contraction coefficients with such quantum side information, including the corresponding strong-data-processing-inequality (SDPI) constants, expansion coefficients, and relative contraction coefficients. For the trace distance, we show that the optimization can be restricted to orthogonal input states. For the quantum relative entropy, we prove that its conditional contraction coefficient is exactly equal to the contraction coefficient of the conditional mutual information, extending the classical correspondence between relative-entropy contraction and mutual-information contraction to the setting with quantum side information. More generally, we identify structural properties of divergences required for these results and discuss extensions beyond the relative entropy. These results establish a unified framework for analyzing information contraction in quantum network settings, where quantum side information and distributed correlations are intrinsic features of the information-processing task. Applications include an extension of the Polyanskiy-Wu bounds on mutual information contraction, new perspectives on mixing times, and fundamental limits on quantum memories.

Abstract: Coherent measurements across multiple copies of an unknown quantum state can substantially reduce the number of samples required to learn its properties, but remain experimentally challenging. Current experiments typically prepare and measure one copy at a time, potentially adapting later measurements to earlier outcomes. A central challenge is adaptivity, which makes the space of possible measurement strategies difficult to characterize. The positive-partial-transpose (PPT) relaxation bypasses this complexity by considering a larger, mathematically tractable class of measurements, at the risk of weakening the resulting bounds. Here we show, surprisingly, that the relaxation loses nothing at the level of asymptotic sample complexity for two fundamental tasks: purity testing and product testing. In both cases, lower bounds against the full class of PPT measurements are matched by nonadaptive single-copy protocols. Moreover, our proof requires only basic symmetric subspace identities, providing a simple route to sharp lower bounds for adaptive single-copy measurements.

Abstract: Single photon emitters in silicon carbide (SiC) are a leading platform for scalable quantum technologies. Recent work has shown that an adversary who pre-distributes entanglement between the devices can mount attacks that are strictly more powerful than those available when the devices share only classical correlations, reducing the extractable randomness below the previously certified rates. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假设最小化的布尔表达式映射到电路即为最优，但忽略了最小化后表达式中仍保留代数结构（如项之间的包含关系和互补极性关系），这些结构可以进一步提取以降低电路执行成本。由于量子纠错硬件的执行成本由最宽控制操作的数量（超线性成本）决定而非操作总数，因此需要重新审视"最小化即最优"的前提。

Method: 本文提出一种表示级变换方法：从最小化的布尔表达式中提取项之间的包含关系（containment）和互补极性（complementary-polarity）等代数结构，通过提取共享因子（shared factors），将少量宽操作（高控制数）转化为大量窄操作（低控制数），从而降低最宽操作的控制数和量子比特数。

Result: 在多个基准测试以及量子搜索和因数分解算法的预言机上，该表示级变换从不增加操作计数或控制数这两个成本度量（由构造保证）。在转换为可执行电路（需对辅助线进行反计算）后，因子化电路仍能比单独运行的领先电路级优化器更快地达到更低的最终计数。

Conclusion: 计算的表示本身是一种可优化的资源，与逻辑最小化和电路级优化不同，可在编译前独立优化。最小化表达式并非最优表示；提取其中隐含的代数结构可以获得执行成本更低的电路，即使操作数增多也是如此。关键的成本度量不是操作数，而是最宽操作的控制数。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms promise advantages beyond classical reach, but running them on error-corrected hardware requires translating Boolean specifications into reversible circuits, and the resources that translation demands determine what is executable. Established methods minimize a Boolean expression and map it to a circuit, assuming the minimized form is best. Here we show that minimized expressions retain algebraic structure minimization cannot reach, arising from containment and complementary-polarity relationships among their terms, and that extracting it yields circuits cheaper to execute despite having more operations. The decisive quantity is not a circuit's operation count but the control count of its widest operation, a superlinear cost; extracting shared factors trades a few wide operations for many narrow ones and reduces qubit count. Across benchmarks and oracles from quantum search and factoring algorithms, at the representation level the transformation never increases either cost measure, a guarantee from its construction. Translation to an executable circuit returns part of that advantage, since auxiliary lines must be uncomputed, yet the factorized circuit still left a leading circuit-level optimizer reaching lower final counts, and faster, than unaided. The representation of a computation is therefore itself a resource, optimizable before compilation and distinct from both logic minimization and circuit-level optimization.

</details>

### [83] [Quantized Transport through a Supermoiré Chern Mosaic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26252)
*Li-Qiao Xia,Aviram Uri,Zachary W. Gomez,Molly P. Andersen,Julian May-Mann,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Trithep Devakul,Yves H. Kwan,Pablo Jarillo-Herrero,Aaron Sharpe*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 魔角螺旋三层石墨烯（MATTG）在零场下会弛豫为携带相反谷分辨陈数的畴结构，边界形成无隙导电网络，但研究发现外加磁场可诱导谷选择性拓扑相变，使各畴的占据态总陈数趋于一致，从而打开全局陈隙，观察到C=-6且霍尔电阻在4.6 K下量化精度达2%的量子化输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔角螺旋三层石墨烯中畴网络的无隙导电模式使得常规量子化输运预期不太可能实现。然而实验中出乎意料地观察到了场致陈隙及量子化霍尔效应，作者旨在解释这一现象背后的物理机制。

Method: 通过实验测量磁场下的霍尔电阻实现量子化输运表征；同时利用Hofstadter模型和轨道塞曼（orbital Zeeman）计算，分析磁场驱动的谷选择性拓扑相变及其对畴结构和陈数的影响。

Result: 在磁场作用下，每个自旋-谷味中占据态的总陈数在相邻畴间变得一致，畴壁模式可以被能隙化，从而形成C=-6的全局陈隙；实验测得霍尔电阻在4.6 K下量化精度达2%（-h/6e²）。尽管中心价带陈数在两类畴间仍有差异，但观测到的量子化输运表明全局能隙存在。

Conclusion: 中等强度磁场可诱导魔角螺旋三层石墨烯发生谷选择性拓扑相变，使不同畴的拓扑性质趋于统一并打开全局陈隙，从而在畴网络系统中实现接近理想的量子化霍尔输运。

Abstract: Magic-angle helical trilayer graphene---three graphene layers sequentially twisted in the same direction by $\sim1.8^\circ$---relaxes into a mosaic of domains that, at zero field, carry opposite valley-resolved Chern numbers, with boundaries hosting a network of gapless conducting modes. Charge transport through this network depends sensitively on how the modes connect and scatter, making well-quantized transport unlikely. Contrary to this expectation, we observe a field-induced Chern gap with Chern number $C=-6$ emanating from charge neutrality; in this gap, the Hall resistance is quantized to within $2\%$ of the expected value, $-h/6e^2$, at 4.6 K. We explain this behavior using both Hofstadter and orbital Zeeman calculations, which show that a moderate magnetic field drives a valley-selective topological transition. Above the transition, the total Chern number of the occupied states in each spin-valley flavor becomes identical across neighboring domains, and the domain-wall modes can become gapped. Though the central valence-band Chern numbers still differ between the two domain types, the observed quantized transport attests to a global gap.

</details>


### [84] [Hidden Frustration in Collinear Altermagnets: Pairing Vortices and Equilibrium Spin Current Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26260)
*Vincent P. Flynn,Benedetta Flebus*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发现中心对称的交错磁体中，对称性允许的局域交错Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya耦合可以在空间动量高点处的反常马农对关联中印入规范不可消除的涡旋-反涡旋对，在实空间中产生围绕相邻格点反向循环的反铁手性自旋流阵列，从而在经典共线磁体中实现完全存在于量子关联中的规范不可消除阻挫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于探索经典共线磁体的量子真空中是否能够承载复杂的拓扑结构（如涡旋、规范通量、平衡环流），从而弥合经典共线序与量子阻挫相之间的鸿沟，展示即使磁矩保持完美共线，量子关联中仍可编码丰富的拓扑与规范结构。

Method: 理论方法包括：(1) 分析中心对称交错磁体中对易的局域交错Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya耦合的对称性约束；(2) 计算高对称点上反常马农对关联的配对函数，揭示其涡旋-反涡旋拓扑荷；(3) 从动量空间涡旋结构映射到实空间的自旋流分布，证明其规范不可移除性。

Result: 结果表明：(1) 在动量空间高对称点处存在规范不可消除的涡旋-反涡旋对，嵌入反常马农对关联（squeezed vacuum）中；(2) 实空间对应反铁手性排列的平衡自旋流，相邻plaquette间流向相反；(3) 尽管磁序保持经典共线，阻挫和量子涡旋完全存在于量子关联层面，而非经典自旋结构之中。

Conclusion: 该项工作将电子相中的复杂配对、规范不变通量和平衡环流等概念系统地移植到共线磁体的squeezed真空态中，确立了量子关联作为拓扑与规范信息载体在共线磁体中的可实现性，为研究规范不可消除阻挫与量子涡旋提供新平台。

Abstract: We show that a magnet can remain perfectly collinear while its quantum vacuum circulates. In a centrosymmetric altermagnet, a symmetry-allowed locally staggered Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya coupling imprints a gauge-irremovable vortex-antivortex pair into the anomalous magnon pair correlations at high-symmetry points in momentum space. In real space, these hidden vortices produce an antiferrochiral array of equilibrium spin currents circulating oppositely around neighboring plaquettes. Gauge-irremovable frustration therefore survives despite classical collinearity: it exists entirely in the quantum correlations. Our results show that complex pairing, gauge-invariant fluxes, and equilibrium loop currents - structures encountered across electronic flux phases, spin liquids, and frustrated quantum magnets - can be encoded in the squeezed vacuum of a collinear magnet.

</details>


### [85] [How quantum is quantum geometry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26269)
*Ady Stern,Felix von Oppen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一个纯粹的经典力学系统——在动量依赖磁场中进动的经典磁矩，发现该系统能复现量子几何张量的全部现象（Berry曲率与量子度规），从而证明许多归因于量子几何的观测量实际上并不需要量子力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何张量（Berry曲率与量子度规）被用来解释从反常霍尔效应到平带超流权重等一系列观测量，但其中哪些真正需要量子力学尚未明确。作者希望探究这些现象中的量子本质，判断是否有纯经典机制可以复现。

Method: 构建一个纯经典哈密顿力学系统：一个携带经典磁矩$\boldsymbol{\ell}$的点粒子，在动量依赖的磁场$\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{p})$中进动。通过分析磁矩沿磁场方向的投影分量和平行进动的横向分量，分别对应Berry曲率现象和量子度规现象。

Result: 该系统在经典哈密顿力学框架下成功复现了量子几何现象：磁矩沿磁场的分量产生Berry曲率现象；进动的横向分量产生量子度规现象。粒子获得了位置展宽（其二阶矩即度规）、轨道磁矩，以及最引人注目的——由位置依赖力产生的惯性质量，进而在标称无色散的系统中获得了非零Drude权重。

Conclusion: 量子几何张量所描述的一系列可观测现象中，有许多可以通过纯经典力学来复现，揭示了这些现象并非本质上的量子效应，而是一般性几何结构的体现。

Abstract: The quantum geometric tensor - the Berry curvature together with the quantum metric - now underlies a long list of observables, from the anomalous Hall effect to the superfluid weight of a flat band. We ask which of these observables actually require quantum mechanics. To answer this question, we study a purely classical system: a point particle carrying a classical magnetic moment $\boldsymbol{\ell}$ that precesses in a momentum-dependent magnetic field $\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{p})$. Within Hamiltonian classical mechanics, the component of $\boldsymbol{\ell}$ along the field reproduces the Berry-curvature phenomena, while its precessing transverse component reproduces the quantum-metric phenomena. The particle acquires a position spread whose second moment is the metric, an orbital magnetic moment, and - most strikingly - an inertial mass generated by a position-dependent force, and with it a nonzero Drude weight in a system that is nominally dispersionless.

</details>


### [86] [Majorana signatures in an asymmetrically coupled quantum dot--topological superconducting nanowire junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26341)
*Levente Máthé,Ioan Grosu,Liviu P. Zârbo,Ionel Ţifrea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究从理论上分析了纳米系统中量子点（QD）与正常导线及两个Majorana束缚态（MBSs）不对称耦合时的量子输运，揭示了导线-QD耦合不对称参数α和偏压不对称参数q对线性电导的重要影响，包括零温下MBSs未杂化时电导只在高通量相位下有限，以及有限温度下电导随磁通相位振荡的周期和极大值位置可由这些参数调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Majorana束缚态是实现拓扑量子计算的关键载体，但实验上识别MBSs的输运特征常受到系统中参数不对称性（如导线-QD耦合不对称和偏压不对称）的影响。这些不对称性在真实实验中普遍存在，却可能显著改变MBSs的输运信号，因此需要系统理论研究这些参数对量子输运的影响。

Method: 采用理论研究方法，构建中心量子点与正常导线和拓扑超导纳米线两端局域的Majorana束缚态不对称耦合的模型，在纳米线中引入可调的磁通。在零温极限下解析求解线性电导，并考虑MBSs未杂化和杂化两种情况；在有限温度下分析线性电导随磁通振幅周期的振荡行为，系统考察耦合不对称参数α和偏压不对称参数q的调控效果。

Result: 零温下，MBSs未杂化时线性电导仅在磁通相位φ=(2n+1)π时有限，尺度为G=2qαe²/[h(α+1)]；MBSs杂化时电导对系统参数呈现复杂依赖。有限温度下，未杂化MBSs时电导以2π为周期随磁通相位振荡，且增大偏压不对称参数q可将电导极大值位置从φ=2nπ移至φ=(2n+1)π；杂化MBSs时，当量子点能级调至费米能级时行为类似，但偏离费米能级时振荡周期变为4π且极大值位置依赖具体参数。

Conclusion: 导线-QD耦合不对称参数α和偏压不对称参数q在真实实验中普遍存在，却会强烈影响MBSs输运特征的识别与观测。因此在实际实验中必须考虑这些不对称性，否则可能导致对MBSs输运信号的误判或遗漏。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of the quantum transport through a nanoscale system in which a central quantum dot (QD) is coupled asymmetrically to normal leads and to two Majorana bound states (MBSs) localized at the ends of a topological superconducting nanowire threaded by a tunable magnetic flux. The effects of the leads--QD coupling asymmetry parameter $α$ and the bias voltage asymmetry parameter $q$ on the system's linear conductance are considered for the case of unhybridized and hybridized MBSs. In the zero-temperature limit, for unhybridized MBSs the system's linear conductance is finite only when the magnetic flux phase $φ= (2n+1)π$ ($n\in\mathbb{Z}$) and it scales as $\mathcal{G}=2qαe^2/[h(α+1)]$, while for hybridized MBSs it presents a complicated dependence on the system's parameters. At finite temperature, for unhybridized MBSs, the system's linear conductance oscillates as a function of the magnetic flux phase $φ$ with a period of $2π$, and the position of the linear conductance maxima can be shifted from $φ=2nπ$ to $φ=(2n+1)π$ by simply varying the value of the bias voltage asymmetry parameter $q$. For hybridized MBSs, the conductance exhibits a similar behavior when the energy level of the central QD, $\varepsilon_d$, is tuned at the leads' Fermi level ($\varepsilon_d=\varepsilon_F$), although when $\varepsilon_d\neq\varepsilon_F$ the oscillation period changes to $4π$, and the position of the linear conductance maxima depends on the actual value of $\varepsilon_d$ and other parameters in the system. Our results highlight the experimental importance of the leads-QD and bias voltage asymmetry parameters, which are often present in realistic experimental setups, and can strongly affect the identification and observation of MBSs transport signatures.

</details>


### [87] [Magnetic Communication with an Acoustically Actuated Magnetoelectric Resonator and a Quantum Diamond Magnetometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26396)
*Christopher T. -K. Lew,Andrew D. M. Charles,Brant C. Gibson,Jean-Philippe Tetienne,David A. Broadway*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并演示了一种混合磁通信系统，采用声学驱动的高效磁电谐振器作为发射器，金刚石氮空位中心量子磁强计作为接收器，在20 kHz交流磁场下实现了50 pT/√Hz的灵敏度和1.2 mrad/√Hz的相位灵敏度，为导电介质（如水下和地下）中的短中程通信提供了传统环形天线方案之外的高效替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁通信系统主要采用环形天线，其灵敏度和频率响应分别受限于线圈截面积的增大和减小，在导电介质（如水下、地下）中基于电场的通信方式效率低下。作者旨在探索一种突破尺寸限制的混合磁通信系统，以解决传统环形天线在灵敏度和尺寸上的固有矛盾。

Method: 构建混合磁通信系统：发射端采用高效辐射、结构紧凑的声学驱动磁电谐振器（Magnetoelectric Resonator），接收端采用基于金刚石氮空位（NV）中心的微米级高灵敏度量子磁强计。该系统在f_AC=20 kHz下进行幅度和相位编码的交流磁场传输与接收实验。

Result: 在20 kHz频率下实现了幅度编码传输，灵敏度达到50 pT/√Hz；同时实现了相位编码传输，相位灵敏度达到1.2 mrad/√Hz。

Conclusion: 该混合磁电谐振器与量子金刚石磁强计通信系统被证实是现有环形天线磁通信方案的一种可行替代方案，其核心器件性能不受尺寸约束，在导电介质短中程通信场景中具有潜在优势。

Abstract: Wireless communication via propagating magnetic fields is a communication modality that has recently garnered significant interest for short-to-medium range communication in conductive mediums, such as underwater and underground, where existing approaches utilizing electric fields are highly inefficient. Typical implementations of magnetic communication make use of loop antennas as both the transmitter and receiver, with the sensitivity and frequency response scaling with and inversely with the loop cross-sectional area, respectively. Here, we explore an alternative hybrid magnetic communication system consisting of an highly radiation efficient and compact acoustically actuated magnetoelectric resonator as the transmitter, and a highly sensitive micrometer scale quantum magnetometer based on nitrogen-vacancy centers in diamond as the receiver, with their core properties unconstrained by size. We demonstrate amplitude and phase-encoded transmission and reception of AC magnetic fields at $f_{\mathrm{AC}}$ = 20 kHz, achieving a sensitivity of 50 pT/$\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ and 1.2 mrad/$\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$, respectively. This work establishes the use of hybrid magnetoelectric resonator and quantum diamond magnetometer communication system as a viable alternative to existing loop-based approaches.

</details>


### [88] [Statistical Disorder in MBE-Grown AlGaAs/GaAs Superlattices for Quantum Bragg Mirrors using Synchrotron X-ray Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26406)
*Mateus T. Souza,Germano M. Penello,Guilherme A. Calligaris,Sergio L. Morelhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化MBE生长AlGaAs/GaAs超晶格中纳米级结构无序度的综合方法。通过高能同步辐射X射线衍射表征203层QBM器件，结合递归动力学衍射模型与集成模拟退火优化，提取了逐层厚度分布，实现了约1-2个原子单层（0.2-0.6 nm）的统计精度，并识别出系统性材料相关的厚度偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QBM红外探测器等光电器件的性能高度依赖于超晶格的近完美周期性和陡峭界面，但MBE生长过程中固有的统计波动会引入纳米级结构无序，从而降低器件效率。因此需要一种能够精确量化和诊断这种无序的方法，以指导MBE工艺优化。

Method: 采用25 keV高能同步辐射X射线衍射对203层QBM器件进行高分辨率结构表征；将递归动力学衍射理论与集成模拟退火精修算法耦合，从衍射数据中提取统计上稳健的逐层厚度剖面。

Result: 所有AlGaAs势垒层均比名义设计薄0.3-0.6 nm，呈现系统性材料特异偏差；序列厚度剖面识别出顶部接触层存在35 nm的显著厚度亏缺，首层GaAs层存在40 nm亏缺；统计精度达到约0.2-0.6 nm（1-2个原子单层）。

Conclusion: 该非破坏性诊断方法能够为MBE通量校准协议提供直接可操作的反馈，有助于优化超晶格器件的生长质量和最终性能。

Abstract: AlGaAs/GaAs superlattices grown by Molecular Beam Epitaxy (MBE) are foundational for advanced optoelectronic devices, including Quantum Bragg Mirror (QBM) infrared detectors. The performance of these devices critically depends on achieving near-perfect periodicity and abrupt interfaces, however, intrinsic statistical fluctuations during MBE growth introduce nanoscale structural disorder that can degrade device efficiency. In this study, we present a comprehensive methodology for quantifying this disorder in a 203-layer QBM device. High-resolution structural characterization was performed using high-energy (25 keV) synchrotron X-ray diffraction. By coupling a recursive dynamical diffraction formalism with an ensemble simulated annealing refinement, we extracted statistically robust, layer-by-layer thickness profiles. Our analysis reveals highly systematic, material-specific deviations from the nominal design: all AlGaAs barrier layers were consistently thinner than nominal by 0.3-0.6 nm. Furthermore, the sequential thickness profile successfully identified a significant 35 nm deficit in the final macroscopic top contact layer and a 40 nm deficit in the first GaAs layer. Achieving a statistical precision of about 0.2 to 0.6 nm (approximately 1-2 atomic monolayers), this non-destructive diagnostic approach provides directly actionable feedback for MBE flux calibration protocols.

</details>


### [89] [Optical spectroscopy of composite fermion edge states in the fractional quantum Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26521)
*Maria Sebastian,Ashutosh Singh,Alexey Belyanin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过复合费米子（CF）平均场框架，理论预测了分数量子霍尔效应样品中边缘态可被亚太赫兹光学光谱选择性探测和激发。CF边缘态吸收峰出现在毫米波至亚太赫兹频段（如GaAs中B=10T时为60-500GHz），相对体态CF回旋频率发生蓝移，但远低于整数量子霍尔回旋频率。吸收谱中每个系列的峰数对应填充的Lambda能级数，可指纹性地标识填充分数。边缘附近的反演对称破缺激活了体态中禁戒的光学跃迁，并在电偶极近似下实现二阶非线性过程。吸收谱的绝对频率尺度由CF有效质量决定，该质量完全由电子-电子相互作用产生，因此该光谱为相互作用诱导质量提供了直接光学探针。

Method: 采用复合费米子（CF）平均场框架，将强关联的分数量子霍尔问题映射为有效的整数量子霍尔问题，计算Jain序列填充分数的吸收光谱，同时包含体态和边缘态的贡献。利用边缘附近反演对称性破缺的特性，分析原本在体态中被禁止的光学跃迁及二阶非线性光学过程。

Result: 计算得到CF边缘态吸收峰位于毫米波至亚太赫兹频段（B=10T的GaAs中约60-500GHz），相比体态CF回旋频率发生蓝移，但显著低于整数量子霍尔回旋频率。每个吸收谱系列的峰数等于填充的Lambda能级数，可直接指纹性地确定填充分数。边缘态允许二阶非线性光学过程，且吸收谱的绝对频率由相互作用产生的CF有效质量决定。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了分数量子霍尔边缘态的光学光谱表征方法，提供了一种直接探测相互作用诱导的复合费米子有效质量的新途径，并为边缘态中的非线性光学研究打开了可能性。

Abstract: We show that edge states in fractional quantum Hall effect samples can be selectively probed and excited with sub-terahertz optical spectroscopy. Using the composite fermion (CF) mean-field framework, which maps the strongly correlated fractional quantum Hall problem onto an effective integer quantum Hall problem, we calculate the absorbance spectrum for the Jain sequence of filling fractions including both bulk and edge states. The CF edge-state absorption peaks appear in the millimeter-wave to sub-terahertz range, e.g., 60-500 GHz at B = 10 T in GaAs, i.e. they are blueshifted with respect to the bulk CF cyclotron frequency but are well below the integer quantum Hall cyclotron frequency scale at the same magnetic fields. The number of resolved peaks in each series of the absorption spectrum counts the filled Lambda-levels and fingerprints the fraction. Inversion symmetry breaking near the edge activates optical transitions forbidden in the bulk and enables second-order nonlinear processes in electric-dipole approximation. The absolute frequency scale of the spectrum is set by the CF effective mass, which is generated entirely by electron-electron interactions, so the absorption spectrum provides a direct optical probe of this interaction-induced mass.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者提出一个通量可调的四终端正常金属-超导结。巧妙之处在于：在恰好半个磁通量子（φ=π）处，Aharonov-Bohm干涉和Peierls干涉分别结构性禁止ET和AR；同时通过调节中心结的跳迁强度使电子反射归零。利用Cauchy辐角原理证明该抑制对应量子化的拓扑缠绕数，从而保证单位CAR概率的拓扑保护。

Result: 实现了100%确定性的CAR效率；证明该区域具有严格正的交叉关联散粒噪声，可作为Cooper对分裂的无歧义实验标志；且该完美CAR在超导能隙内近乎宽带，对结构无序具有显著鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方案为确定性非局域纠缠生成提供了高度鲁棒的架构，有望推动固体量子信息处理中非局域纠缠源的实际应用，并为实验上隔离和验证纯CAR过程提供了明确的拓扑保护机制和可观测的实验信号。

Abstract: Generating non-locally entangled electron pairs via Cooper-pair splitting is vital for solid-state quantum information processing. However, isolating the underlying crossed Andreev reflection (CAR) is challenging due to competing transport processes like electron tunneling (ET) and local Andreev reflection (AR). Here, we propose a flux-tunable four-terminal normal metal-superconductor junction that achieves deterministic, 100\% efficient CAR. We demonstrate that at exactly half a magnetic flux quantum ($φ=π$), exact destructive Aharonov-Bohm and Peierls interferences structurally forbid ET and AR respectively. By tuning the central junction hopping, electron reflection is also suppressed to zero. Using the Cauchy argument principle, we prove that this suppression manifests as a quantized topological winding number, guaranteeing a topologically protected unit CAR probability. We establish that this regime is characterized by a strictly positive cross-correlation shot noise, providing an unambiguous experimental signature of Cooper-pair splitting. Furthermore, this perfect CAR is nearly broadband within the superconducting gap and remarkably robust against structural disorder, offering a highly resilient architecture for deterministic nonlocal entanglement generation.

</details>

Result: 实验直接观测到了30°扭转双层WSe₂中的复制带，其对称性和强度分布可由从底层到顶层WSe₂的层间Umklapp散射解释；谱函数测量表明在K谷附近存在van Hove奇点。

Conclusion: 该研究首次确定了二维准晶中多轨道十二重复制带的电子结构和散射过程，证实了van Hove奇点邻近K谷的存在，为大扭转角双层体系中诱导电子重构提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Twisted bilayers of two-dimensional (2D) transition metal dichalcogenides are promising systems for achieving tunable quasicrystalline orders with emergent properties. The underpinning electronic structure and scattering processes in 2D quasicrystals with multiorbital dodecagonal replica bands have so far not been determined. Here, we utilize angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) with micrometer spatial resolution to directly observe replica bands in 30$^{\circ}$-twisted bilayer WSe$_2$. The symmetry and intensity distribution of the observed replicas are explained by interlayer Umklapp scattering from bottom to top WSe$_2$ layers. Our spectral function measurements are consistent with the presence of a van Hove singularity adjacent to the $\mathrm{K}$-valleys, which underlines the possibility of inducing electronic reconstructions in bilayers with a large interlayer twist angle.

</details>

Method: 采用双离子栅控（double-ionic gated）双层石墨烯器件，通过双重离子栅极施加极强的电场来控制对称性破缺，从而在室温下实现异常强烈的反演对称性破缺，并系统研究了器件的NLHE响应及其可切换特性（开启、关闭、符号反转）。

Result: 实验观察到可切换（开、关以及符号反转）的NLHE，其二阶非线性磁化率χ^(2)_yxx达到室温下的巨大值3×10⁻³ μm·S/V，与WTe₂或石墨烯莫尔超晶格在低温下观测的值相当，比此前用于追求室温NLHE的材料体系高出3-4个数量级；对应的太赫兹电压响应度约4×10⁴ V/W，可与商用肖特基二极管相媲美。

Conclusion: 双离子栅控双层石墨烯的性能比此前报道的室温NLHE器件高出数个数量级，使其成为无结非线性技术的重要候选平台，为实用化室温非线性器件提供了极具前景的材料方案。

Abstract: Utilizing quantum second-order nonlinear transport for practical junction-free devices require materials with large and tunable nonlinearites at room temperature -- a current materials platform challenge. Here, we report the nonlinear Hall effect (NLHE) in double-ionic gated bilayer graphene devices that enable unusually strong inversion breaking. We observe NLHE that are readily switchable (on, off, and sign reversed) with second order nonlinear susceptibilities $χ^{(2)}_{yxx}$ that reaches giant room-temperature values of $3\,10^{-3}\,μ\mathrm{m}\,\mathrm{S/V}$, comparable to values commonly observed at low temperature in WTe$_2$ or in graphene-based moiré superlattices, and three-to-four orders of magnitude larger than values reported in material systems recently employed in search of a room-temperature NLHE. Our devices produce corresponding THz voltage responsivities $\simeq 4\,10^{4}\,\mathrm{V/W}$, comparable to commercially available Schottky diodes. These are orders of magnitude better than for previously reported room-temperature NLHE devices rendering double-ionic gated bilayer graphene a choice platform for junction-free nonlinear technology.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文证明各向异性核壳纳米颗粒的径向静电问题无需增益/损耗或非互易性即可实现奇异点（EP），通过介电各向异性作为唯一控制参数，在无源、互易、无损耗的壳层中实现非厄米相变，超越双曲性阈值后电场由单调衰减变为空间振荡，产生可用于荧光调控、强耦合、传感或隐身的中间半径热点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统奇异点（EP）需要精心设计的增益-损耗平衡或非互易性来实现，这增加了实验复杂度。作者希望探索一种更简单的方案：仅通过介电各向异性就能在被动系统中实现EP，从而开辟非厄米物理模拟的新途径。

Method: 将各向异性核壳纳米颗粒的径向静电问题写入对数径向坐标，转化为Cauchy-Euler方程，进而映射到阻尼谐振子及两格点Hatano-Nelson哈密顿量。非厄米性由空间（而非时间）模拟，唯一控制参数是介电各向异性比ε_t/ε_r，系统的EP和非厄米相变仅由双曲性（hyperbolicity）决定。

Result: 该被动、互易、无损耗的壳层中存在EP及非厄米相变。超过双曲性阈值后，壳内电场从单调衰减转变为空间振荡，在中间半径产生热点，可应用于荧光调控、强耦合、传感或隐身。

Conclusion: 这项工作确立了双曲介质作为无需增益、损耗或非互易性即可模拟非厄米物理的平台，为EP物理在无源纳米光子学中的应用提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are non--Hermitian degeneracies at which both eigenvalues and eigenvectors coalesce, usually engineered through balanced gain and loss or non--reciprocity. In this work, we show that the radial electrostatic problem of an anisotropic core--shell nanoparticle realizes an EP without any of the alluded conditons. Written in the logarithmic radial coordinate, the problem is a Cauchy--Euler equation that maps onto a damped oscillator and, equivalently, onto a two-site Hatano--Nelson Hamiltonian, so that the non-Hermiticity is emulated in space rather than in time and the sole control parameter is the degree of dielectric anisotropy, $\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon_r$. The shell is passive, reciprocal, and lossless, yet it hosts an EP and a non--Hermitian phase transition governed by hyperbolicity alone. Beyond a hyperbolicity threshold the electric field inside the shell turns from monotonic decay into spatial oscillation, producing hotspots at intermediate radii which can be exploited to tailor fluorescence, strong coupling, sensing, or cloaking. More broadly, this establishes hyperbolic media as a platform to simulate non--Hermitian physics without gain, loss, or non--reciprocity.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超洁净导体中，快速粒子间碰撞可形成粘性电子流体并实现水动力学输运态。为了理解和预测这种水动力学输运机制下的磁输运行为，尤其是在具有不同粗糙度边缘的二维电子系统中的表现，需要建立相应理论模型。

Method: 开发了二维电子水动力学磁输运理论，通过一个具有速度量纲的单一参数在样本边缘边界条件中描述任意粗糙度，并在此框架下计算长样本中的电子流体流动剖面以及相应的纵向电阻和霍尔电阻。

Result: 霍尔电阻中与弛豫过程相关的贡献在高磁场极限下饱和；对于足够粗糙的边缘，该贡献随磁场表现出最小值，且此最小值随边缘粗糙度减小、样本宽度增大或缺陷体散射增强而消失。

Conclusion: 霍尔电阻的这些特征可作为实验上识别电子水动力学输运态的标志，并可用来确定水动力学参数，特别是边缘粗糙度的大小。

Abstract: In ultra-clean conductors, fast inter-particle collisions can lead to the formation of a viscous electron fluid and realization of the hydrodynamic transport regime. Here we develop a theory of hydrodynamic magnetotransport of two-dimensional (2D) electrons in samples with low densities of defects and edges of arbitrary roughness. Within our model the roughness is described by a single parameter with the dimension of speed in the boundary conditions on sample edges. The electron-fluid flow profiles in long samples, as well as the corresponding longitudinal and Hall resistances, are calculated. The contribution to the Hall resistance associated with the relaxation processes exhibits a saturation in the limit of high magnetic field and a minimum as a function of the magnetic field for sufficiently rough edges. The minimum disappears as the edge roughness decreases or the sample width and bulk scattering by defects increase. These properties of the Hall resistance can serve as the signs to identify the hydrodynamic regime of electron transport in experiments and can be used to determine its parameters, in particular, the degree of edge roughness.

</details>

*Jonas Bloch,Fedor Baranov,Maxim Breitkreiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有离散螺旋对称性的长分子中自旋保持增强的机制，证明即使驻留时间远超自旋混合时间尺度，自旋仍可通过准动量中的强自旋分离得以保持，并提出了自旋阀实验签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长分子电子输运中，通常认为只有当驻留时间远短于自旋混合时间尺度ℏ/Δ时自旋才能被保持。然而这一传统观点与手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）实验现象存在矛盾，因为许多CISS实验涉及长手性分子，其驻留时间已超过自旋混合尺度。因此需要理解在长分子中自旋保持为何仍能实现。

Method: 作者提供了增强自旋保持的解析推导，并在紧束缚模型上进行了数值验证。研究中系统地测试了螺旋对称性被破坏或从离散变为连续时效应如何减弱。此外，进行了输运模拟以展示在双磁电极自旋阀结构中对称保护自旋保真度产生的强磁阻特征。

Result: 解析推导和紧束缚模型验证一致表明：具有离散螺旋对称性的分子中，自旋在准动量空间被强烈分离，使得自旋保持可以远超ℏ/Δ时间尺度而得以增强。该效应在对称性被破坏或变为连续螺旋对称时逐渐减弱。输运模拟还显示自旋阀装置中会出现由对称保护自旋保真度诱导的强磁阻特征。

Conclusion: 该研究为长手性分子中的CISS效应提供了对称性保护的微观机制，将离散螺旋对称性与自旋保持增强联系起来，并提出磁阻测量作为可实验验证的指纹特征。这一发现深化了对手性分子中自旋依赖输运的理解，并为CISS机制的实验甄别提供了可操作的方案。

Abstract: In electronic transport through long molecules, spin is expected to be preserved only when the dwell time is much shorter than the characteristic spin-mixing timescale $\hbar/Δ$, where $Δ$ is the magnitude of a spin-dependent potential, such as spin-orbit coupling. We show that, in molecules featuring discrete screw symmetry, spin preservation can be enhanced far beyond this timescale owing to strong spin separation in quasi-momentum. This spin fidelity in long molecules is consistent with chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS), suggesting spin-dependent transport in long, chiral molecules with amplified spin-splitting mechanisms. We provide analytical derivation of the enhanced spin preservation and test it on tight-binding models, which confirm that the effect gradually weakens when the screw symmetry is broken or changes from discrete to continuous. Furthermore, we perform transport simulations to show that a strong magnetoresistance trace of symmetry-protected spin fidelity emerges in a spin-valve setup with two magnetic leads, which we propose as an experimentally accessible signature.

</details>


### [96] [Band's Geometry Origin of Quantum Spin Transport Phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27445)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋相关输运是自旋电子学的核心问题，以往研究多从拓扑能带论或贝里曲率等角度出发，缺乏对费米面几何结构与自旋输运之间内在联系的系统性描述。本文旨在建立费米面几何与自旋输运之间的直接联系。

Method: 利用局部几何结构（等能面的双曲区域）对费米速度进行几何贡献分解，揭示其与电子自旋的自然耦合；在时间反演对称性约束下，建立自旋算符代数与能带切空间外代数之间的同构关系；基于辛几何框架描述费米面上的自旋分离输运，并通过接触几何将其推广至三维能带流形。

Result: 证明了准二维系统中等能面双曲区域产生几何费米速度贡献，该贡献与自旋耦合并产生自旋流响应；建立了自旋算符代数与能带切空间外代数的几何对应关系；提出了费米面上自旋分离输运的辛几何描述及其三维推广。

Conclusion: 费米面几何与自旋输运之间存在直接的、内禀的联系，本文建立的几何框架为理解自旋相关输运提供了新的理论视角，并可用于描述包括自旋流产生在内的自旋电子学现象。

Abstract: We develop a geometric description of spin-dependent transport based on the local geometric structure of electronic bands and the Fermi surfaces. For quasi-two-dimensional systems, we show that hyperbolic regions of constant-energy surfaces generate a geometrical contribution to the Fermi velocity that couples naturally to electron spin and produces a spin-current response. We further show that, in the presence of time-reversal symmetry, the algebra of spin operators can be related to the exterior algebra of the band's tangent space, providing an additional geometric interpretation of spin in momentum space. This framework motivates a symplectic description of spin-separated transport on Fermi surfaces and its extension to three-dimensional band manifolds through contact geometry. Our results establish a direct connection between Fermi-surface geometry and intrinsic spin transport.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频人工声子晶体为片上集成提供了低损耗平台，但在GHz频率下实现陈声子相长期具有挑战性，需要新的物理机制来打破时间反演对称性并打开完整的陈带隙。

Method: 在蜂巢声子晶体的六边形中心引入铁磁岛，形成磁子-声子杂化系统。圆极化Kittel模式与周围声子产生带相位缠绕的耦合，从而打破时间反演对称性。在大失谐区域，该机制可映射为具有磁子诱导复跳跃的有效Haldane型声子模型。通过调节磁声子相互作用强度来调控拓扑性质和陈数。

Result: 该系统可进入陈数|C|=1和|C|=2的可调陈相，所预测的带隙超过实际声子和磁子线宽，可在GHz声学器件中观测。展示了手性磁子-声子杂化作为实现磁性可重构拓扑声子学的有效途径。

Conclusion: 手性磁子-声子杂化机制为在GHz频率实现可调拓扑声子相提供了新平台，有望推动磁性可重构拓扑声子学的发展。

Abstract: High-frequency artificial phononic crystals offer a low-loss platform compatible with on-chip integration, yet realizing Chern phononic phases at GHz frequencies remains challenging. Here, we propose a magnon-induced phononic Chern insulator in a honeycomb phononic crystal hybridized with ferromagnetic islands at the hexagon centers. A circularly polarized Kittel mode couples to the surrounding phonons with a phase winding, which breaks time-reversal symmetry and opens a full Chern gap. In the large-detuning regime, this mechanism leads to an effective Haldane-type phononic model with magnon-induced complex hopping. By tuning the magnon-phonon interaction, the full hybrid system accesses Chern phases with tunable Chern numbers |C|=1 and |C|=2. The predicted gaps can exceed realistic phonon and magnon linewidths, enabling their observation in GHz acoustic devices. Our work establishes chiral magnon--phonon hybridization as a route to magnetically reconfigurable topological phononics.

</details>


### [98] [Giant bulk photovoltaic effect driven by interfacial symmetry breaking in MoS2/Ta2NiSe5 heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27064)
*Jianwen Ma,Pengliang Leng,Lei Peng,Congming Hao,Xianghao Meng,Jiaqi Liu,Yang Gan,Min Luo,Zifan Zhang,Jiaming Gu,Qinghang Liu,Lidan Duan,Du Xiang,Wu Shi,Peng Wang,Weibin Chu,Xiang Yuan,Weida Hu,Cheng Zhang*

TL;DR: 本文展示了MoS2/Ta2NiSe5十字交叉范德华异质结中局域化于重叠区域的显著体光伏（BPV）效应。通过正交器件几何结构独立探测层内与界面光响应路径，利用栅压和垂直电场调控层间势垒，实现了优化的零偏置光电流密度247 A/cm²和BPV系数0.99 V⁻¹，为分析优化界面驱动BPV效应提供了新策略。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华异质结构虽能通过界面对称性破缺实现非常规体光伏效应，但多重光物理机制（结构复杂度、自发电荷转移、强层间耦合）的共存常常掩盖BPV响应的微观起源，阻碍了理性优化。

Method: 构建MoS2/Ta2NiSe5十字交叉范德华异质结构，利用正交器件几何独立探测层内和界面光响应路径；通过垂直堆叠的对称性破缺消除MoS2的反演中心；利用自发界面电荷转移建立强层间电子耦合；通过栅极电压和垂直电场调制层间势垒景观来优化BPV响应。

Result: 在重叠区域观测到显著的BPV效应，实现了247 A/cm²的零偏置光电流密度和0.99 V⁻¹的BPV系数，并通过理论建模验证了实验结果。

Conclusion: 极简器件几何结构可将复杂异质结构转化为实验上可处理的平台，为分析与优化界面驱动BPV效应开辟新途径，对自供电光电器件、宽带光探测和能量收集纳米器件具有重要意义。

Abstract: Van der Waals (vdW) heterostructures offer a versatile platform for engineering unconventional bulk photovoltaic (BPV) effect through interfacial symmetry breaking. However, the coexistence of multiple photophysical mechanisms, driven by structural complexity, spontaneous charge transfer, and strong interlayer coupling, often obscures the microscopic origin of the BPV response and hinders its rational optimization. Here, we demonstrate a pronounced BPV effect localized at the overlap region of a cross-bar MoS2/Ta2NiSe5 vdW heterostructure, where symmetry breaking induced by vertical stacking lifts the inversion center of MoS2. The orthogonal device geometry enables the independent probing of intralayer and interfacial photoresponse pathways, facilitating clear separation of competing mechanisms. Spontaneous interfacial charge transfer between MoS2 and Ta2NiSe5 further establishes a strong interlayer electronic coupling. By modulating the interlayer potential landscape through gate voltage and vertical electric fields, we achieve an optimized zero-bias photocurrent density of 247 A/cm2 and a BPV coefficient of 0.99 V-1. Supported by theoretical modelling, our results illustrate how minimalist device geometry can transform complex heterostructures into experimentally tractable platforms. This strategy paves the way for analyzing and optimizing interface-driven BPV effect, with implications for self-powered optoelectronics, broadband photodetection, and energy-harvesting nanodevices.

</details>


### [99] [Terahertz anomalous Hall effect in magnetic Weyl semimetal Co$_3$Sn$_2$S$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27195)
*Ashutosh Singh,Hongjing Xu,Xielin Wang,Kohei Fujiwara,Atsushi Tsukazaki,Shengxi Huang,Andrey Baydin,Junichiro Kono,Alexey Belyanin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文通过太赫兹光谱结合半解析有效模型计算，系统研究了Co₃Sn₂S₂的Weyl半金属本征低能旋光（gyrotropic）光学响应，揭示了动量空间中Weyl节点分离引起的本征旋光性，并通过实验与理论的定量比较对材料参数施加了严格约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演对称性破缺的Weyl半金属在零频极限下其反常霍尔电导与节点间的动量空间分离成正比。尽管Co₃Sn₂S₂中已观测到巨大的反常霍尔效应，但在直接探测Weyl节点附近准粒子激发的低能区域，实验研究仍然有限，因此需要对这一低能光学响应进行系统性研究。

Method: 采用太赫兹光谱实验与基于物理直观有效模型的半解析计算相结合的方法，系统研究Co₃Sn₂S₂的低温本征旋光光学响应。

Result: 实验结果揭示了由Weyl节点动量空间分离产生的本征旋光性导致的磁光现象；实验与理论的定量比较为材料参数提供了严格的约束条件。

Conclusion: 该研究为Co₃Sn₂S₂中观测到的磁光现象提供了稳健而透明的物理解释，确认其源于Weyl节点的动量空间分离所产生的本征旋光性，并通过定量对比实验和理论精确限定了材料参数。

Abstract: Time-reversal-symmetry-broken Weyl semimetals are known to have at least two nodes in their electronic band structure, separated in momentum space and acting as sources and sinks of Berry curvature. This gives rise to a transverse Hall conductivity, known as the anomalous Hall effect (AHE), which, in the simplest two-node picture, is proportional to the momentum-space separation between the nodes in the zero frequency limit. In the recently discovered Weyl semimetal $\mathrm{Co_3Sn_2S_2}$, a giant AHE has been observed. However, experimental investigations in the low-energy regime, which directly probe quasiparticle excitations near the Weyl nodes, remain limited. Here, we present a systematic study of the intrinsic low-energy gyrotropic optical response of $\mathrm{Co_3Sn_2S_2}$ using terahertz spectroscopy combined with semianalytical calculations based on a physically intuitive effective model. Our results provide a robust and transparent explanation of the observed magnetooptical phenomena in terms of intrinsic gyrotropy arising from momentum-space separation of the Weyl nodes. Furthermore, quantitative comparison between experiment and theory places stringent constraints on the material parameters.

</details>


### [100] [Occupation-Driven Josephson Diode in a Symmetric Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27213)
*Jianxiong Zhai,Zelei Zhang,Jiawei Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约瑟夫森二极管机制，其非互易性并非源于传统的不对称Andreev谱，而是通过外部储层工程实现的非平衡占据。在双量子点结中，相位纹理化的非局域储层充当量子Zeno选择器，通过快速耗散冻结亮态，保留正交的暗Andreev通道，从而在保留超流的同时实现Josephson二极管效应。稳态电流可分解为I_ss=I_A P_γ，即使Andreev电流严格互易，P_γ的相位不对称性也能独立产生二极管效应。局域库仑排斥可通过暗对共振将系统驱动至近理想二极管态，Keldysh-Lindblad计算证明有限超导能隙和耗散下的结论依然稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Josephson二极管依赖不对称Andreev谱，本文旨在探索一种全新的非互易性来源——通过储层工程实现电流载流态的非平衡占据，从而在不依赖谱不对称的情况下产生Josephson二极管效应，为超导非互易输运提供新的物理机制。

Method: 采用双量子点约瑟夫森结模型，引入相位纹理化的非局域储层作为量子Zeno选择器，通过Keldysh-Lindblad形式理论计算稳态电流。在无限能隙极限下推导出电流分解公式I_ss=I_A P_γ，并分析局域库仑排斥对暗对共振和二极管效率的影响，最后扩展到有限超导能隙和耗散的真实结情形进行稳健性验证。

Result: 证明储层工程可独立于Andreev谱产生Josephson二极管效应；量子Zeno效应通过耗散冻结亮态、保留暗Andreev通道从而维持超流；局域库仑吸引可通过暗对共振将系统推向近理想二极管态；有限能隙和耗散下结论依然稳健。

Conclusion: 本文确立了一种全新的Josephson二极管机制：通过非平衡占据而非谱不对称实现非互易性，拓展了Josephson二极管的设计空间，为利用储层工程调控超导量子输运提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We propose a Josephson diode mechanism in which nonreciprocity arises not from a conventional asymmetric Andreev spectrum but from nonequilibrium occupation of the current-carrying states engineered by attached reservoirs. We realize this mechanism in a double-quantum-dot junction, where a phase-textured nonlocal reservoir acts as a quantum Zeno selector: rapid dissipation freezes out the bright state directly coupled to the jump operator $L$, while preserving an orthogonal dark Andreev channel whose supercurrent remains comparable to that of the lossless junction. In the infinite-gap limit, the steady-state current factorizes as $I_{\rm ss}=I_A P_γ$, so that even when the Andreev current $I_A$ is strictly reciprocal, the phase asymmetry of $P_γ$ alone can produce a Josephson diode effect through reservoir engineering. We further show that local Coulomb repulsion can drive the system toward a nearly ideal diode regime via a dark-pair resonance. Using Keldysh-Lindblad calculations, we demonstrate that our results remain robust for realistic junctions with a finite superconducting gap and dissipation.

</details>


### [101] [Mobility Enhancement in Si/SiGe Quantum Well Enabled by a Buried Si Layer Trapping Oxygen Impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27377)
*Felix Reichmann,Alberto Mistroni,Fabian Fidorra,Giovanni Capellini,Yuji Yamamoto,Marco Lisker,Marvin H. Zoellner*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出在Si/SiGe异质结构中引入一层薄型电中性掩埋Si层，可将氧背景浓度降低约5倍，同时提高电子迁移率，但不改变渗流密度和密度依赖的迁移率标度，是一种简便、工艺兼容的异质结构设计策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序是Si/SiGe场效应异质结构量子器件（尤其是电子自旋量子比特）可扩展化的关键材料挑战。氧等背景杂质已被确认是限制迁移率的因素，但如何通过实际的异质结构设计策略来抑制其掺入仍缺乏探索，且其在不同输运机制下的影响尚未完全明确。

Method: 在200 mm Si(100)衬底上采用减压化学气相沉积（RP-CVD）生长Si/SiGe量子阱异质结构，在下层SiGe势垒中引入一层薄型电中性掩埋Si层。使用二次离子质谱（SIMS）表征氧背景浓度，并通过密度与温度依赖的磁输运测量评估电子迁移率、渗流密度及密度依赖的迁移率标度等输运特性。

Result: SIMS显示掩埋Si层可复现地将后续生长的SiGe中氧背景降低约5倍，且不影响有源量子阱区域。氧背景的降低提升了电子迁移率，而渗流密度和密度依赖的迁移率标度基本保持不变。在0.3 K温度下，高氧和低氧器件均表现出相似的密度依赖分数迁移率增强，表明氧背景降低主要改善了动量弛豫，而未显著改变主导低密度无序面貌。

Conclusion: 掩埋Si层是一种直接的、工艺兼容的异质结构设计元素，可有效降低氧掺入并改善200 mm CVD生长的Si/SiGe量子器件材料的输运性能，为可扩展量子器件材料工程提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reducing disorder in undoped Si/SiGe field-effect heterostructures remains an important materials challenge for scalable quantum devices, particularly electron spin qubits. Background impurities such as oxygen have been identified as mobility-limiting, yet practical heterostructure-design strategies for suppressing their incorporation remain underexplored, and their influence across different transport regimes is not fully established. Here, we demonstrate a simple route to oxygen reduction and mobility enhancement in Si/SiGe quantum-well heterostructures grown by reduced-pressure chemical vapor deposition (RP-CVD) on 200 mm Si(100) substrates through the introduction of a thin, electrically passive buried Si layer within the lower SiGe barrier. Secondary-ion mass spectrometry shows that the buried Si layer reproducibly reduces the oxygen background in the subsequently grown SiGe by approximately a factor of five, without modifying the active quantum-well region. Density- and temperature-dependent magnetotransport measurements further show that this reduction increases the electron mobility, while leaving the percolation density and density-dependent mobility scaling largely unchanged. Upon cooling to 0.3 K, both high- and low-oxygen devices exhibit similar density-dependent fractional mobility enhancements, indicating that the reduced oxygen background improves momentum relaxation without substantially altering the dominant low-density disorder landscape. These results establish the buried Si layer as a straightforward and process-compatible heterostructure-design element for reducing oxygen incorporation and improving transport in 200 mm CVD-grown Si/SiGe quantum-device materials.

</details>


### [102] [Scalable, Simple, and Versatile Encapsulation of 2D Materials and Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27383)
*Gabriel Natale,Uma Chirkova,Flávio Henriques Feres,Ran Jing,Michael Geiwitz,Wenyao Liu,Emma Low,Josh Leeman,Kyung-Mo Kim,Leslie M. Schoop,Mohamed Shehabeldin,Qiong Ma,Michael A. Susner,Pijush Bhattacharya,Genda Gu,Katherine Lee,James Hone,Mengkun Liu,Kenneth S. Burch*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用电子束蒸发的氧化铝（AlO_x）作为空气敏感二维材料的封装层，该方法兼具有效封装与直接器件制备功能，适用于厚薄片和全晶圆覆盖，无需堆叠或蚀刻步骤，并在多种二维材料上验证了其抑制氧化、保持本征光电性质的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气敏感二维材料在器件集成中面临根本性挑战，通常需要封装来保持本征性质，但传统保护策略对较厚层效果不佳且使制备工艺复杂化。亟需一种既能有效封装又兼容简单器件制备的方法。

Method: 采用电子束蒸发沉积AlO_x作为封装层和直接器件制备平台。该方法为可扩展的物理气相沉积方案，无需转移/堆叠步骤，无需封装后蚀刻即可形成接触；通过对RTe3、WTe2和FeTe_xSe_1-x等材料的系统测试，建立不同厚度flake的衬底依赖优化方案。

Result: AlO_x封装有效抑制了多种空气敏感二维材料（RTe3、WTe2、超导FeTe_xSe_1-x）的氧化，保持了本征光学和电子性质；超薄AlO_x保留了WTe2的等离激元响应并维持了FeTe_xSe_1-x的超导性能。

Conclusion: 该方法克服了脆弱量子材料中"有效封装"与"简便器件制备"之间长期存在的权衡矛盾，为厚层和全晶圆级别集成提供了可行的规模化途径。

Abstract: Air-sensitive 2D materials present a fundamental challenge for device integration. Encapsulation is often required to preserve intrinsic properties, yet conventional protection strategies often fail for thicker layers and complicate fabrication. Here, we demonstrate that electron-beam (e-beam) evaporated aluminum oxide ($\mathrm{AlO}_x$) serves as both an effective encapsulation layer and a platform for direct device fabrication. Unlike transfer-based approaches, this scalable method is compatible with thicker flakes and full device or wafer coverage. It requires no stacking procedures and enables contacts without post-encapsulation etching. Using rare-earth tritellurides ($\mathrm{RTe}_3$, R = La, Er), semimetallic $\mathrm{WTe}_2$, and superconducting $\mathrm{FeTe}_x\mathrm{Se}_{1-x}$, we show that $\mathrm{AlO}_x$ suppresses oxidation and preserves intrinsic optical and electronic properties. We establish substrate-dependent optimization of encapsulation across a range of flake thicknesses, demonstrate that ultrathin $\mathrm{AlO}_x$ preserves $\mathrm{WTe}_2$'s plasmonic response and maintains superconducting performance in $\mathrm{FeTe}_x\mathrm{Se}_{1-x}$. Thus we overcome the longstanding tradeoff between encapsulation and straightforward device fabrication in fragile quantum materials.

</details>


### [103] [Trapping $e/4$ quasiparticles in bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27444)
*Mario Di Luca,Emily Hajigeorgiou,Ning Ma,Alexandra Waldherr,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Mitali Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过在双层石墨烯中使用栅极定义的量子点（antidot）测量了偶数分母分数量子霍尔态中准粒子的电荷。研究发现ν=-5/2、-1/2和3/2态中局域化电荷为e/4，与主要候选非阿贝尔基态的最小激化一致；在空穴共轭态ν=2/3中观测到e/3电荷。增加量子点与扩展边缘态的耦合会驱动两种输运机制之间的交叉，该结果支持双层石墨烯中偶数分母FQH态具有非阿贝尔基态，且准粒子可局域化于量子点周围，为拓扑量子计算提供了必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量偶数分母分数量子霍尔态所容纳准粒子的电荷对于确定其基态的拓扑性质至关重要，这是区分非阿贝尔候选态与常规态的关键一步。

Method: 在双层石墨烯中构造一个栅极定义的量子点，并通过额外的栅极仅控制量子点势，利用库仑阻塞/隧穿谱技术测量被困在量子点周围的准粒子电荷。通过调节量子点束缚态与扩展边缘态之间的耦合强度，研究两种输运机制之间的交叉行为。

Result: 在ν=-5/2、-1/2和3/2三个偶数分母态中观测到局域化电荷e/4，在ν=2/3观测到e/3。增加耦合后观察到输运机制的交叉，表现为最小激化栅压周期与约两倍周期之间的转变。

Conclusion: 偶数分母FQH态在双层石墨烯中的表现与非阿贝尔基态一致，准粒子可实现局域化，为拓扑量子计算提供了必要前提。

Abstract: Measuring the charge of the quasiparticles hosted by even-denominator fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states is essential to identify the topology of their ground state. Here, we use a gate-defined antidot in bilayer graphene, with an additional gate to control only the antidot potential, to measure the charge of the quasiparticles trapped around it in even-denominator FQH states. We observe a localized charge of $e/4$ at $ν=-5/2$, $-1/2$, and $3/2$, consistent with the minimal excitation expected for leading candidate even-denominator ground states, and $e/3$ at the hole-conjugate state $ν=2/3$. We further show that increasing the coupling between the antidot-bound states and extended edge states drives a crossover between two regimes, characterized by the minimal-excitation gate-voltage period and approximately twice that period, respectively. We discuss two possible explanations for this crossover: quasiparticle bunching and a crossover between distinct antidot transport regimes. Our results, together with previous observations of the daughter states, show that the even-denominator FQH states in bilayer graphene are compatible with a non-Abelian ground state, and that their quasiparticles can be localized around a quantum Hall antidot, a necessary ingredient for topological quantum computation.

</details>


### [104] [Distinguishing Quantum Capacitance Signatures of a Topological Majorana Wire from a Normal Wire Segment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27452)
*Binayyak Bhusan Roy,Jay Deep Sau,Sumanta Tewari*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析表明，拓扑超导（TS）线中马约拉纳零模（MZMs）产生的量子电容响应不同于无间隙正常线段的赝马约拉纳响应。两者的关键区别在于：拓扑线中偶/奇宇称两条低能分支近似简并、始终处于同一量子点势共振区域，而无间隙正常线段中两条宇称依赖信号源自分离的能量共振。因此，可通过量子电容响应在参数空间（量子点势和化学势）的持续存在性来区分真实MZM与赝马约拉纳信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来已有研究表明，无间隙（Δ=0）的正常线片段可通过Aharonov-Bohm干涉重现类马约拉纳的磁通依赖量子电容振荡，这严重混淆了基于量子点干涉仪探测MZMs的实验结果。因此，需要找到一种能够在实验中区分真正的马约拉纳零模信号与赝马约拉纳响应的可靠判据。

Method: 作者对两种机制（拓扑超导线中的MZMs与无间隙正常线中的Aharonov-Bohm干涉）分别分析了量子点干涉仪中量子电容响应的谱学特征。具体比较了偶/奇宇称能量共振的相对位置与简并性：在无间隙正常线中，两条宇称信号源自分离的能量共振；在拓扑线中，两条宇称能级近似简并，保持在同一个量子点势共振区域内。通过考察量子电容响应在量子点势和导线化学势独立调节下的行为来判定其来源。

Result: 两种机制的量子电容磁通痕迹在单一优化点上可能相似，但其在参数空间中的持续性和稳定性本质不同。拓扑线中偶/奇宇称分支始终处于同一共振区域，响应在量子点势和化学势的有限变化范围内持续存在；而无间隙正常线中两条宇称信号分离，不具备这种参数空间稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，判断量子电容响应是否真正来源于MZMs的关键不在于某一特定磁通迹线，而在于该响应是否在有限的独立控制参数（尤其是量子点势）范围内持续存在。这一参数空间稳定性判据可直接排除无间隙正常线片段作为类马约拉纳响应的来源。

Abstract: Majorana zero modes (MZMs) are spatially separated, near-zero-energy excitations expected at the ends of a topological superconducting (TS) wire. A quantum-dot interferometer can be used to probe the quantum capacitance of the TS wire, and the location and magnitude of the capacitance resonances provide information about the MZMs, while their magnetic flux dependence probes coherent coupling to the two Majorana modes. It has been recently shown that, a gapless (i.e., $Δ=0$) wire segment can also exhibit flux-dependent quantum capacitance oscillations through Aharonov-Bohm interference and, with suitable tuning, can reproduce a Majorana-like response. Here we show that the two mechanisms can be distinguished experimentally. In the gapless normal wire segment, the two parity-dependent signals originate from separate energy resonances corresponding to the lack of generic zero-energy states. By contrast, in the topological wire, the pair of low energy levels with even and odd parity are nearly degenerate in energy, and therefore, the two parity branches remain within the same broader resonance region in the quantum-dot potential, and persist under independent variations of the dot potential and wire chemical potential. Our results show that the experimentally observed quantum capacitance response is distinguished not by a particular flux trace at one optimized point in parameter space, but by its persistence over a finite range of independently controlled parameters, including the quantum-dot potential. This parameter space stability provides a direct means of ruling out the gapless normal wire segment as the origin of the observed Majorana-like response.

</details>
