### [105] [Dynamical phase selection controls compute scaling in looped transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26556)
*Gunn Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文揭示了循环Transformer的推理动力学存在不同的动力学相，这些相由初始化决定，并决定了测试时计算量的缩放规律。不同相通过不同的分岔机制（如鞍结分岔和Neimark-Sacker分岔）区分，其中鞍结相可推导出无参数标度关系τ(ε)[1-λ_max(-ε)]→π，而Neimark-Sacker相中该标度律消失。测试时计算量并非仅由架构决定，而是由训练所找到解的动力学相所决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过迭代权重共享映射进行推理，其计算成本由推理动力学过程决定。此前人们可能默认相同架构和训练目标收敛到相同表现的模型具有类似的计算特征，但本文希望探究训练结果在动力学层面的多样性，特别是初始化是否会导致截然不同的动力学相，以及这些相如何影响测试时计算量的标度。

Method: 通过理论和数值相结合的方法：首先对循环Transformer的推理迭代过程进行动力学分析，识别不同分岔机制（鞍结分岔与Neimark-Sacker分岔）；在鞍结相中，利用一维法式（normal form）约化，从训练映射的局部导数预测弛豫时间和谱隙振幅，导出无参数关系τ(ε)[1-λ_max(-ε)]→π；并将问题难度分布与临界慢化结合，推导出工作负载级尾部P(τ>N)~N^{-2}的标度。

Result: 研究发现即使架构和目标函数完全相同、训练准确度一致，不同初始化会使网络落入截然不同的动力学相。鞍结分岔相满足τ(ε)[1-λ_max(-ε)]→π的无参数关系和尾部标度P(τ>N)~N^{-2}；而Neimark-Sacker相中该标度律完全消失而非仅改变前缀因子。这表明测试时计算量与架构无关，由训练解的动力学相决定。

Conclusion: 测试时计算量不由架构单独决定，而是由训练所找到的动力学相所支配。相变机制（分岔类型）决定了推理时间尺度的缩放行为。这一发现对理解循环模型推理效率、以及如何通过初始化等训练因素控制推理计算成本具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: A looped transformer performs inference by iterating a weight-tied map, making its computation a dynamical process whose cost is set by the resulting inference dynamics. Here we show that networks with identical architecture and objective, trained to identical accuracy, nevertheless realize distinct dynamical phases depending strongly on initialization, and that the bifurcation defining each phase determines how test-time compute scales. The phases are distinguished by their bifurcation mechanisms, including a saddle-node fold and a Neimark-Sacker-type transition to bounded nonstationary motion. In the fold phase, a one-dimensional normal-form reduction predicts both the relaxation-time and spectral-gap amplitudes from local derivatives of the trained map, yielding the parameter-free relation $τ(\varepsilon)[1-λ_{\max}(-\varepsilon)]\toπ$. Composed with a regular distribution of problem difficulty, the same critical slowing down produces the workload-level tail $P(τ>N)\sim N^{-2}$. In the Neimark--Sacker phase, the fold scaling law disappears rather than merely changing its prefactor. Thus, test-time compute is not determined by architecture alone. It is governed by the dynamical phase of the solution found by training.

</details>


### [106] [Neural Renormalization Group Flow for Percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26764)
*Anaclara Alvez,Luca Camagna,Sergio Chibbaro,Cyril Furtlehner,François Landes,Gianluca Manzan,Lorenzo Mensi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于二维渗流问题的有监督、跨尺度共享的神经网络架构，通过递归应用相同的粗粒化规则实现数据驱动的实空间重正化，在仅用小晶格训练的情况下能外推到更大系统，并准确恢复跨越团簇和临界点附近的有限尺寸标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习为数据驱动的实空间重正化提供了可能途径，尤其当相关观测量是非局域的且难以显式预设时。传统方法需要明确指定相关可观测量，而渗流问题中的跨越概率等非局域观测量使得这一任务具有挑战性。

Method: 开发了一个监督式的、尺度共享（scale-shared）的神经架构，用于二维位点渗流。模型递归地在不同尺度上应用同一个学习到的粗粒化规则，产生一个潜在场（latent field），从中预测跨越概率；同时通过对应的细粒化解码器重建最大团簇掩码。

Result: 模型仅在小晶格上训练，却能外推到显著更大的系统，以高保真度恢复跨越团簇，并产生符合临界点附近预期有限尺寸标度的可观测量。关键发现是，所学习的潜在表征必须表现出临界涨落和与渗流重正化群结构一致的尺度依赖流，才能获得如此性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作表明，通过精心设计的尺度共享神经架构，学习到的潜在表征可以自发捕获重正化群的结构特征，从而使得小系统训练能够有效外推到大规模系统，为数据驱动的实空间重正化提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Machine learning offers a possible route to data-driven real-space renormalization when the relevant observables are nonlocal and difficult to prescribe explicitly. We explore this idea for two-dimensional site percolation developping a supervised, scale-shared neural architecture. The model recursively applies the same learned coarse-graining rule across scales, producing a latent field from which the crossing probability is predicted, while a corresponding fine-graining decoder reconstructs the largest-cluster mask. Trained only on small lattices, the model extrapolates to substantially larger systems, recovers the spanning cluster with high fidelity, and produces observables obeying the expected finite-size scaling near the critical point. We observe that to get such performance it is key that the learned latent representation exhibits critical fluctuations and scale-dependent flows consistent with the renormalization-group structure of percolation.

</details>


### [107] [Low-temperature magnetism and spin dynamics in the disordered triangular-lattice Yb$^{3+}$ compound LiCaYb$_5$(BO$_3$)$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26946)
*Monika Jawale,Saikat Nandi,Prashanta K. Mukharjee,Philipp Gegenwart,A. V. Mahajan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文报道了无序三角格子化合物 LiCaYb5(BO3)6 (LCYBO) 的低温磁性及 7Li NMR 研究。该材料具有部分 Ca/Yb 反位无序和分数 Li 占据的 P6522 六方结构，表现出 J_eff=1/2 Kramers 双重态、弱反铁磁相互作用（θ_CW ≈ -0.54 K）以及低温下短程关联磁性而非长程有序的特征。7Li NMR 谱随冷却显著展宽，自旋-晶格弛豫呈双组分开裂，表明反位无序和竞争交换路径导致的局域磁环境分布。LCYBO 被确立为具有关联低能自旋动力学的结构无序阻挫三角格子磁体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索几何阻挫、自旋-轨道耦合与结构无序三者相互作用如何在稀土三角格子磁体中产生非常规磁基态，从而拓展对无序阻挫磁系统低能物理的理解。

Method: 结合 Rietveld 精修结构分析、磁化率与磁化强度测量、比热测量以及 7Li 核磁共振（NMR）谱学（包括谱线形状和自旋-晶格弛豫）等实验手段，对 LCYBO 的晶体结构与低温磁行为进行系统表征。

Result: 确认了 P6522 六方结构及 Ca/Yb 反位无序；磁测量显示 J_eff=1/2 Kramers 双重态及弱反铁磁相互作用；比热在 0.43 K 出现弱异常（短程关联而非长程有序）；7Li NMR 谱低温下显著展宽，自旋-晶格弛豫呈现两个组分为无序诱导的局域磁环境分布。

Conclusion: LCYBO 是一种具有关联低能自旋动力学的结构无序阻挫三角格子磁体，其无序与阻挫的协同作用抑制了长程磁有序并产生不均匀的局域磁环境。

Abstract: The interplay between geometric frustration, spin--orbit coupling, and structural disorder can give rise to unconventional magnetic ground states in rare-earth triangular-lattice magnets. We report low-temperature magnetic and $^7$Li NMR investigations of the disordered triangular-lattice compound LiCaYb$_5$(BO$_3$)$_6$ (LCYBO). Rietveld refinement confirms a hexagonal $P6_522$ structure with partial Ca/Yb antisite disorder and fractional Li occupancy. Magnetic susceptibility and magnetization measurements indicate a well-isolated effective $J_{\mathrm{eff}}=1/2$ Kramers doublet with weak antiferromagnetic interactions ($θ_{\mathrm{CW}} \approx -0.54$~K). The specific heat reveals a weak anomaly near 0.43 K, suggesting the development of short-range correlated magnetism rather than conventional long-range order. $^7$Li NMR spectra broaden strongly upon cooling, consistent with increasingly inhomogeneous internal magnetic fields. The spin-lattice relaxation exhibits two relaxation components consistent with disorder-induced distributions of local magnetic environments arising from antisite disorder and competing exchange pathways. Our results establish LCYBO as a structurally disordered frustrated triangular-lattice magnet with correlated low-energy spin dynamics.

</details>
